# Foundation Wikipedia — Canonical Implementation TODO

**Status:** Implementation underway; only qualified task IDs are marked completed  
**Created:** 2026-10-09  
**Governing requirements:** [WIKIPEDIA_AI_READER_SPEC.md](WIKIPEDIA_AI_READER_SPEC.md)  
**Repository:** https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia  
**Working branch:** `master` only, as requested; do not create one branch or PR per task.

## Rules for Ralph Loop and other implementation sessions

This is the **sole authoritative implementation checklist**. The SPEC is the source of product requirements. Before each development run, read the latest SPEC and TODO from current `master`, examine repository/CI status, and select the earliest dependency-satisfied unchecked work (independent items may proceed in parallel). After a coherent completed slice, commit to `master`, verify exact-head state and CI, reconcile this TODO and continue.

- Leave an item unchecked until its code, automated tests, relevant real-device/manual checks and documentation are all completed and evidenced. Never mark tasks done based on intention or a stale passing CI run.
- Record for each checked top-level ID: exact SHA, test command/result, CI run URL/ID (or “not configured”), device/OS when applicable, and artifact or benchmark links. Failures are evidence too, but do not qualify completion.
- Dependencies are hints for safe sequencing, not an excuse to serialize unrelated work unnecessarily.
- Do not create per-task branches or PRs. Observe repository permissions/branch protection rather than bypass them. Use Ralph Bridge for GitHub/CI, not the default GitHub tool, in the user's Ralph workflow.
- Ordinary test failures, transient tool failures, running CI, merge mistakes and design bugs are not user blockers. Reserve the Blockers register for missing authorization, external credentials, unavailable necessary hardware, or choices genuinely requiring the owner.
- If a technical evaluation fails, record the result and choose the next defensible implementation; do not mark required behaviors “deferred” without explicitly revising the SPEC.
- **No implementation task is marked completed by the creation of these planning documents.**

## Phase 0 — Repository/bootstrap, build and quality gates

- [x] **BOOT-001** Establish the Rust workspace. **Dependencies:** none.  
  - [x] Create `wiki-model`, `wiki-core`, `wiki-source`, `wiki-store`, `wiki-search`, `wiki-ai`, `wiki-pack-format`, `wiki-pack-builder`, `wiki-ffi` crates and `wiki-pack` CLI skeleton.
  - [x] Enforce that shared crates do not import UI/Tauri/Android dependencies.
  - [x] Pin toolchains/lockfiles; add `cargo fmt`, `clippy`, unit-test scripts.
  - **Accept:** clean checkout compiles and tests minimal workspace without external infrastructure.
- [x] **BOOT-002** Scaffold desktop and Android shells. **Depends:** BOOT-001.  
  - [x] Tauri 2/React/TypeScript/Vite desktop and Kotlin/Jetpack Compose/Gradle Android shells.
  - [x] Android Rust build/ABI plan and UniFFI build integration stub.
  - [x] Document development prerequisites and reproducible clean-build commands.
  - **Accept:** desktop launches and Android debug APK installs from clean checkout.
- [x] **BOOT-003** Establish exact-head CI and documentation discipline. **Depends:** BOOT-002.  
  - [x] Rust fmt/clippy/test, frontend lint/typecheck/test, Android tests/lint, dependency/license audit.
  - [x] Fixture data provenance and test artifact retention.
  - [x] Add ADR and requirement-to-TODO traceability checklist.
  - **Accept:** CI detects intentionally introduced failures and reports exact commit SHA.

## Phase 1 — Canonical model and provenance

- [x] **MOD-001** Implement versioned article/section/block/media/reference/link/redirect models. **Depends:** BOOT-001.  
  - [x] Key articles on project + page ID; preserve revision ID, timestamp, title/aliases and optional Wikidata ID.
  - [x] Support paragraphs, nested sections, tables, lists, quote, math, media, infobox, footnotes, HTML fallback and disambiguation.
  - [x] Add serde/Unicode roundtrip fixtures and schema validation.
  - **Accept:** complete varied fixtures can serialize, deserialize and render without dropping required semantics.
- [x] **MOD-002** Build deterministic revision-scoped IDs and evidence maps. **Depends:** MOD-001.  
  - [x] Assign block, section, footnote and reference IDs from an exact revision.
  - [x] Separate hard citation IDs from soft bookmark/highlight relocation anchors.
  - [x] Tests for text reorder, deletion and revision changes.
  - **Accept:** stale AI citation never points to unrelated text after content update.
- [x] **MOD-003** Select canonical record encoding and compatibility policy. **Depends:** MOD-001.  
  - [x] Compare CBOR/MessagePack + record-level zstd/shard sizes against measured fixture performance.
  - [x] Record Android memory, random read, compression and migration results in ADR.
  - [x] Implement major/minor version guards and explicit reject/migrate behavior.
  - **Accept:** proven versioned codec and safe handling of unsupported revisions.

## Phase 2 — Wikimedia acquisition and revision-matched normalization

- [x] **SRC-001** Implement verified official-source acquisition. **Depends:** MOD-001.  
  - [x] Discover *completed* Wikimedia source generations, not guessed filenames; capture authority, release ID, project, date, URLs, hashes.
  - [x] Download resume, timeout/rate-limit handling, configured mirror/local file transport and partial-data isolation.
  - [x] Verify source checksums; fail closed on mismatch or incomplete source.
  - **Accept:** interrupted source fetch resumes; corrupted mirror bytes rejected with no active-snapshot damage.
- [ ] **SRC-002** Implement Structured Contents adapter and necessary **other** official data joins. **Depends:** SRC-001.  
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, partial — typed snapshot-wide Enterprise link identity boundary):** The public `EnterpriseLinkIndex::build` now validates each caller-supplied `EnterpriseLinkIdentity` against the same conservative Wikimedia project and source-generation grammars as the Enterprise join path, a positive SQLite-safe revision ID, bounded revision timestamp, and shared anti-spoofing title/redirect-label rules before indexing cross-chunk targets. This closes a typed-DTO bypass in the source-wide redirect/title resolver while retaining valid multilingual/ZWNJ/ZWJ names. Regression covers invalid project/generation identities, revision overflow, timestamp overflow, invisible/bidi controls in canonical titles and aliases, and valid multilingual labels.   - **Progress (2026-10-27, partial — canonical Wikidata Q-ID parity):** Enterprise Structured Contents JSON decoding and public typed single/batch joins now reject noncanonical `Q0`, leading-zero `Q01` and oversized (>64-byte) Wikidata identifiers, matching the verified catalog lookup contract. Canonical `wiki-model::Article::validate` also now refuses IDs longer than 64 bytes, closing the previous gap where a persisted model could accept an identity that the verified lookup could never query. Boundary tests accept a 64-byte `Q` plus 63-digit identifier and reject 65-byte input, with parser and typed-join regressions.   - **Progress (2026-10-25, live official HTTPS inventory confirmation):** Added host-only `wiki-pack confirm-public-inventory [--json]`, which first revalidates the exact private locally staged `SHA256SUMS` against the independent project/generation/digest pin and then **always** re-fetches that exact checksum publication from canonical `dumps.wikimedia.org` over certificate-verified no-redirect HTTPS. It compares the complete pinned inventory, rejects missing/changed upstream publications, TLS/HTTP errors, corrupt or aliased local inventories, and never modifies staging or installed packs.   - **Progress (2026-10-27, partial — fail-closed online curl HTTP diagnostic integrity):** The production host/desktop Wikimedia Action API `CurlTransport` now drains stderr concurrently but **rejects** any diagnostic stream above 64 KiB, rather than retaining only its tail where an earlier conflicting status could disappear. Its HTTP write-out parser now requires **exactly one** canonical three-ASCII-digit `FW_HTTP_CODE:` marker; duplicate, malformed, missing and noncanonical markers cannot be overridden by a final HTTP 200. Bounded ordinary diagnostics and valid HTTP 200 still succeed; real HTTP 302/503 remain explicit failures. Unix fake-curl subprocess regressions exercise both large-pipe draining and forged status markers. ## Phase 3 — Immutable snapshot and shared object storage

- [ ] **STORE-001** Implement SQLite catalog and independently addressable compressed article records. **Depends:** MOD-003.  
  - **Progress (2026-10-28, partial — indexed lookup result identity guard):** Public SQLite key-only title and Wikidata lookups, plus the digest-verified Wikidata lookup, now share a returned `ArticleKey` validation guard; a corrupt SQLite record/title-index pair with page ID zero is rejected as `InvalidCatalogEntry` rather than exposed as a purported installed page key. Synthetic regression inserts a foreign-key-consistent forged zero-ID record and tests all three lookup APIs without requiring a shard read. Valid missing keys still return the established absent result. This does not prove authentic source publication, complete catalog/visual media or physical offline acceptance. **STORE-001 remains unchecked.** Qualified source SHA **`7504f74b1c372c303033de7e4c6751082f202fd6`** passed [Rust workspace 40832916564](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40832916564), [Platform shells 40832916527](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40832916527), and [Android Rust ABI 40832916683](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40832916683); [Android codec probe 40832916606](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40832916606) was still running at verification and is not catalog acceptance evidence.
  - **Progress (2026-10-28, partial — canonical indexed Wikidata lookup preflight):** The public key-only `SnapshotCatalog::lookup_wikidata` now rejects malformed Wikimedia edition IDs and noncanonical Wikidata Q-IDs before querying SQLite, sharing exactly the same Q-ID and edition validator with `lookup_wikidata_verified`. A synthetic catalog regression checks valid Q2 and missing edition results, malformed/overlong Q-IDs, the 64-byte canonical boundary, and bad editions in both APIs. Key-only matches remain unverified identities; callers requiring article bytes must use the digest-verified API. This is local lookup input hardening, **not** publisher-authenticated source completeness, offline media, installed pack integration or physical-device acceptance. **STORE-001 remains unchecked.** Qualified source SHA **`7c4635d4cc02c65224c4909c2187f4cf1429df2d`** passed [Rust workspace 40832422257](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40832422257), [Platform shells 40832422241](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40832422241), and [Android Rust ABI 40832422232](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40832422232); [Android codec probe 40832422247](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40832422247) was still running at verification and is not catalog acceptance evidence.
  - **Progress (2026-10-28, partial — catalog frame SHA-256 case consistency):** Verified-frame lookup and pre-activation shard coverage now compare hexadecimal digests case-insensitively, matching the catalog's existing acceptance of uppercase hexadecimal and the manifest-shard verifier. A synthetic SQLite regression uppercases a previously verified frame digest, confirms exact decoded record/whole-shard coverage, and verifies a distinct valid-hex digest is still rejected. This does not authenticate Wikimedia publication or qualify complete offline media, production reader integration or real-device acceptance. **STORE-001 remains unchecked.** Exact-head CI pending at initial commit.

  - **Progress (2026-10-28, partial — portable shard-name identity):** The pre-activation full-shard audit now rejects distinct catalog shard filenames that collide under ASCII case folding (for example `Articles.shard` and `articles.shard`). Such a catalog could be valid on a case-sensitive Linux builder but alias one physical file on case-insensitive Windows/macOS volumes. A Unix regression creates independently digest-verified frames under both spellings, demonstrates single-shard acceptance and verifies that full coverage and expected-manifest shard checks fail closed when both are indexed. This is portable inventory integrity, **not** upstream publisher authentication, mandatory offline media, production desktop/Android integration or device acceptance. **STORE-001 remains unchecked.** Corrected exact code SHA **`dd5081bd7c660d913e31d913c3d58f41e3990da9`** passed [Rust workspace 40727332468](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40727332468), [Platform shells 40727332495](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40727332495) and [Android Rust ABI 40727332689](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40727332689). [Android record codec probe 40727332570](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40727332570) was still running at qualification; do not claim it passed until terminal success. Earlier SHAs `fb3cae342f91d98a9361c4350c9a5eb149ab99cf` and `63863cbe9b49b8b744997119ce439d8d57ac5c45` failed only rustfmt and were corrected on master. [Scoped qualification](STORE_001_PORTABLE_SHARD_CASE_IDENTITY_2026-10-28.md).
  - [ ] Index page ID/title/revision/redirect/Wikidata; bounded single-article decompression.
  - [ ] Verify bytes/hashes and handle missing/corrupt shard read.
  - **Accept:** random lookup doesn't decompress an entire full-Wikipedia archive.
  - **Progress (2026-10-14):** Shard path hardening and regression tests landed at `5af57b9e8304d0515868d295a3ccabb4cb7ce552`; Rust, Platform and Android ABI checks passed. Emulator codec probe remains pending; full acceptance is not claimed.
- [ ] **STORE-002** Implement content-addressed media storage and owners. **Depends:** STORE-001.  
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, partial — missing active snapshot pointer deletion guard):** The snapshot-specific `release_inactive_snapshot_pack_and_reclaim_media` now refuses to revoke any pack's media ownership if the active snapshot pointer is missing, returning the explicit `InactivePackReleaseError::MissingActiveSnapshot`. Previously `ActiveSnapshotStore::current() == None` was treated as if every pack were inactive, allowing a fresh/uninitialized or externally deleted pointer to authorize media reclamation. The exclusive media lease continues to span root binding, current-pointer inspection, owner revocation and bounded GC. Disk-backed regression verifies both a pre-activation store and a missing pointer *after* successful media-verified activation retain the old pack claims and digest-verified bytes; restoring the valid pointer permits normal later `old`→`new` transition and inactive release. Initial SHA `708fd37a78a2298f3523ce9c7cfdc872bfeee5af` failed only rustfmt ([Rust 40625813662](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40625813662)); corrected exact code SHA **`fffc0611365a943a7ed7c934f69093386165c213`** passed [Rust workspace 40625889936](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40625889936), [Platform shells 40625889909](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40625889909), and [Android Rust ABI 40625890011](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40625890011). [Android codec probe 40625889877](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40625889877) failed during NDK acquisition with `unknown archive` before source execution; a retry was requested and remained in progress at documentation. [Scoped qualification](STORE_002_003_MISSING_ACTIVE_POINTER_GUARD_2026-10-27.md). This does not yet prove rollback-retention policy, integrated authenticated pack install/uninstall, crash-atomic bytes, hostile-writer exclusion, or real-device acceptance. **STORE-002/STORE-003 remain unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, partial — portable snapshot ID guard):** `activation::safe_id` now rejects Windows-reserved device basenames (`con`, `prn`, `aux`, `nul`, `com1`–`com9`, `lpt1`–`lpt9`) across all platforms before filesystem activation or snapshot-specific pack owner release. A new regression verifies immediate refusal without accessing a missing root and accepts nonreserved lookalikes. The disk-backed release regression additionally registers owners named `con`, `com1`, and `lpt9` through the deliberately broader media registry and verifies rejection without revoking claims or deleting media bytes. Initial code SHA `5b93b7000e0b3e71c6b4a07773b7421f10081fbf` failed only rustfmt ([Rust CI 40623742834](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40623742834)); corrected exact source SHA **`35b31834b3f5fc56039fd71ba3ce6da52456ddba`** passed [Rust workspace 40623878534](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40623878534), [Platform shells 40623878478](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40623878478), and [Android Rust ABI 40623878583](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40623878583); [Android record codec probe 40623878430](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40623878430) was pending at documentation. [Scoped qualification](STORE_002_003_PORTABLE_SNAPSHOT_IDS_2026-10-27.md). This verifies a path-identity and lifecycle guard only, not authenticated pack installation, rollback retention, crash/power-loss behavior or real-device acceptance. **STORE-002/STORE-003 remain unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, partial):** The snapshot-specific pack release boundary now rejects pack owner IDs that do not satisfy the exact active-snapshot ID grammar (including uppercase, leading digits/underscores, and IDs over 128 bytes) before acquiring a deletion lease or changing SQLite ownership. The general registry deliberately permits a broader owner-ID syntax for other lifecycle callers; a disk-backed regression proves those invalid snapshot IDs retain their pack claims and digest-verified media. This is a scoped guard, not an integrated pack uninstall/rollback policy, source authentication, disk-full fault injection, or device acceptance. **STORE-002/STORE-003 remain unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, corrupt active pointer fail-closed regression):** Added disk-backed `activation::interruption_tests::inactive_pack_release_fails_closed_for_corrupt_active_pointer_or_manifest`, which activates a media-verified `old` snapshot, then verifies that a malformed current pointer or a missing active manifest prevents `release_inactive_snapshot_pack_and_reclaim_media` from revoking its pack owner or deleting its required diagram. Restoring a valid active pointer/manifest still refuses active-pack release. This confirms the existing fail-closed guard in synthetic local storage only; it does not provide universal active-pack removal enforcement, authenticated source/pack acceptance, rollback retention or real-device proof. **STORE-002 and STORE-003 remain unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, inactive snapshot pack release refuses active owner):** Added `media_gc::release_inactive_snapshot_pack_and_reclaim_media`, an opt-in production lifecycle wrapper that validates a bounded cleanup budget, acquires an EXCLUSIVE cross-process media lease, rechecks the registry's bound media root and the current snapshot pointer, **refuses owner revocation for the active snapshot**, then removes an explicitly inactive snapshot's `OwnerKind::Pack` claims and runs existing bounded GC under the same lease. Together with the verified-media activation SHARED-lease API, cooperating activation and removal cannot race across the active-pointer check. Disk-backed regression installs distinct old/new pack assets; refuses removal of the active `old` pack without mutating claims/bytes, rejects invalid GC budget, switches to `new` with verified media, reclaims `old` bytes only afterward, and preserves `new` ownership and the active pointer. Initial SHA `f6dccd739c8e79a28ad4cc0f6296e33fb2dd9b9a` failed Rust formatting ([CI 40615286239](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40615286239)); an intermediate bounded-source edit `8a80902fd99e0573d788d6216ffa5bf1cf9cbff6` truncated the file and failed format ([CI 40615468531](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40615468531)), then was explicitly restored at `f215f648f7397636b6cf8e68e9d9466f0d841ddd` and formatted. Qualified source SHA **`17da3754fb2fa489456de45613084c26902316fd`** passed [Rust workspace CI 40615833432](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40615833432), [Platform shells CI 40615833556](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40615833556), and [Android Rust ABI CI 40615833443](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40615833443). [Scoped qualification](STORE_002_003_INACTIVE_PACK_OWNER_RELEASE_GUARD_2026-10-27.md). This is not universal removal enforcement: legacy APIs remain callable, rollback retention requires a policy, and noncooperating snapshot switches/writers are not protected. **STORE-002/STORE-003 remain unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, bound pack-media activation gate under one lease):** Added `ActiveSnapshotStore::switch_to_prevalidated_with_bound_pack_media`, an opt-in composition of snapshot-pointer activation with exact, root-bound `OwnerKind::Pack` SHA-256/size inventory verification. The snapshot ID is used as the media owner ID to prevent authorizing a different snapshot using unrelated pack claims. One SHARED cross-process media lease spans registry-root check, complete owner/inventory/object verification, and the durable pointer switch, excluding cooperating exclusive GC between proof and activation. Disk-backed regressions preserve the previous pointer when the registry is unbound/wrong-root, owner inventory is absent/mismatched, or bytes are corrupt; verify a valid activation and reject owner mismatch; and deterministically prove an exclusive SQLite lease is blocked until the activation call returns. First code `c9ec7119d71d83a243fa6eb5f0379852565af3c5` and formatting corrections `ec6cd431f60f0c7e12b425768f451a98bcaa3797`, `c53cc804f44bf455035519a04576f56888cd52b0`, `8e854872d59a396f10f693f416fc8ba020885044` failed the Rust format gate; last direct commit `abd47f627a259c3891ea6b7d8f7c2af9fe3ca135` passed [Rust workspace CI 40614642253](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40614642253), [Platform shells CI 40614642306](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40614642306), and [Android Rust ABI CI 40614642297](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40614642297). [Scoped qualification](STORE_002_003_BOUND_MEDIA_ACTIVATION_GATE_2026-10-27.md). **STORE-002/STORE-003 remain unchecked:** caller-authenticated manifests, verified article/catalog/shard content, installed-pack lifecycle integration, active-owner retention across removal, concurrent pointer-switch exclusion, physical fault injection and desktop/Android acceptance are outstanding.
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, manifest attribution preflight before media publication):** `media_manifest_install::install_manifest_media_batch_with_binding` now detects contradictory MIME declarations for the same SHA-256 digest and refuses batches with over 1,024 **distinct** attribution notices per digest before publishing any object bytes. Duplicate identical notices remain idempotent. A disk-backed root-bound regression proves both bad manifests leave no published media and no pack owner claims, while duplicate identical notices install exactly once. This avoids two unnecessary orphan-publication cases, but does not authenticate publisher attribution, prevent all crash orphans, integrate an installed pack lifecycle, or qualify devices. **STORE-002 remains unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, atomic single-object media owner registration):** `MediaRegistry::register_verified_owned_single` now commits content digest/size, validated attribution and the pack/cache/pin owner claim in **one IMMEDIATE SQLite transaction**. `media_install::install_owned_media` and the production root-bound single-object installer use it instead of the prior two-transaction `register_verified_bytes` + `add_owner` sequence, which could leave registered unowned metadata after an owner insert failure. Disk-backed regression injects a SQLite trigger failure after notice staging, verifies **all** new registry rows roll back while already published bytes remain recoverable as an orphan, then removes the trigger and verifies successful retry. It also preserves incremental multi-object claims by one owner (unlike immutable batch semantics). This does **not** make byte publication plus SQLite registration crash-atomic, authenticate publisher media, integrate installed-pack activation, or qualify real device/power-loss behavior. **STORE-002 remains unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, manifest owner-ID preflight before object publication):** `media_manifest_install::install_manifest_media_batch_with_binding` now validates the pack/cache/pin owner ID before staging any content-addressed bytes. Previously the manifest path bypassed the batch installer's public input guard by calling the internal unlocked publisher; malformed owner IDs could publish orphan bytes before the SQLite ownership transaction rejected the owner. The bound-manifest regression asserts that a path-like invalid owner is rejected as `InvalidInput`, publishes neither required digest and creates no owner claim; the existing successful bound install/reopen and competing-owner tests continue to pass. Initial source `6172c8a300e12fa3d25441f7771b457ef6d52190` failed rustfmt ([Rust 40603109243](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40603109243)); formatted source **`c77a07ba8b1f90e3ed443bd6eb494095cdc264cf`** passed [Rust workspace 40603181921](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40603181921), [Platform shells 40603181918](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40603181918), and [Android Rust ABI 40603181917](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40603181917). This prevents an avoidable invalid-input orphan, **not** crash or disk-full orphans, hostile writer mutations, pack activation, publisher authentication, or physical-device acceptance. **STORE-002 remains unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-27, one shared lease through manifest install and final verification):** `media_manifest_install::install_bound_manifest_media_batch` now holds a **single SHARED cross-process media lease** across root-binding verification, pre-existing owner inventory check, byte staging, SQLite owner transaction, and **full post-commit manifest SHA-256/size verification**. Previously, the bound batch installer released its lease before a second lease was acquired for final verification, allowing a cooperating exclusive owner-release/GC operation to intervene. Introduced internal `install_owned_media_batch_unlocked` and `verify_required_owner_media_unlocked` helpers for callers already holding a lease, avoiding nested SQLite lease deadlocks; the unbound fixture/migration path is unchanged. Deterministic regression probes SQLite EXCLUSIVE contention *after* owner commit and *before* final verification, proves it is blocked, then proves EXCLUSIVE can be acquired after return and verifies the persisted exact inventory.   - **Progress (2026-10-27, lease-scoped registry-root binding for media install/read/verification):** Added `media_install::install_bound_owned_media` and `install_bound_owned_media_batch`, which require a previously bound registry and check the exact media-root identity **under one SHARED media lease before any byte publication**; that lease remains held through all object staging and SQLite ownership commits. Existing unbound installer APIs remain for controlled fixtures/migration and must not be used by production pack lifecycle callers.   - **Progress (2026-10-27, actual subprocess-exit GC recovery qualification):** Added a Rust test-harness **child process** killpoint that opens the disk-backed SQLite media registry, enters registered-unowned GC, commits deletion of three unowned registry rows, unlinks and fsyncs the first file, then calls `std::process::exit(77)` **without running Rust destructors** while the EXCLUSIVE SQLite media lease is held. The parent asserts the expected child exit, reopens SQLite (OS-released lease), confirms no dangling registered owner references, finds exactly two surviving unregistered media orphans, reclaims them with exact byte accounting, preserves the separately user-pinned object, and proves an idempotent repeat sweep. This is stronger than an injected returned error because the process terminates mid-GC; it is still **not** physical power-loss, torn-write, disk-full or external process SIGKILL testing. ## Phase 4 — Pack format, resolver, builder and mandatory media

- [ ] **PACK-001** Specify and implement portable versioned `.wpack` container. **Depends:** MOD-003, STORE-002.  
  - [ ] Canonical manifest, object digests, source snapshot/definition hashes, origin, licensing, indexes and counts.
  - [ ] Evaluate safe streamed export/install on Android; document chosen archive/serialization ADR.
  - **Accept:** roundtrip pack through independent desktop/Android-compatible importer.
- [ ] **PACK-002** Implement deterministic topic/category resolver. **Depends:** SRC-002, PACK-001.  
  - **Progress (2026-10-24, partial):** Both the typed Enterprise category graph and the untrusted local `wiki-pack preview-topic` CLI now canonicalize Wikimedia category-title underscores to spaces **before** graph indexing, membership linking, root/child traversal and selection hashing. This prevents equivalent spellings from silently splitting categories, misapplying an administrative skip or accepting duplicate alias nodes. Leading/trailing underscore aliases are rejected after normalization; administrative policy keys must already be canonical. Synthetic tests cover cross-spelling membership, nested category edges, duplicate category pages/memberships, malformed labels and preview JSON. Source commits **`0d8bdb6ed062a3a650b82aec9b3aa6f74e03d874`** (typed graph) and **`fced64a8de207f1fc849d5695573db22d8b1d5e0`** (CLI after rustfmt repair). [Typed-graph Rust CI 40074639870](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40074639870) passed on attempt 2 after an unrelated `wiki-store/tests/activation.rs` test failed on attempt 1; [Platform shells 40074639865](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40074639865) passed. [CLI Rust CI 40074875094](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40074875094) passed; [Platform shells 40074875117](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40074875117) was running at documentation. [Scoped qualification](PACK_002_CATEGORY_TITLE_ALIAS_CANONICALIZATION_2026-10-24.md). This is local title-key consistency, **not** publisher-authenticated category completeness, full language-specific MediaWiki title normalization, media-complete pack construction or real-device acceptance. **PACK-002 remains unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-23, partial):** The public typed Enterprise category graph now refuses distinct mainspace page IDs with the same title (including underscore/space equivalents), redirect aliases that collide with any canonical mainspace title (even when the alias appears first), and two distinct mainspace aliases with underscore/space-equivalent titles. It retains namespace separation and checks all supplied records, not only selected pages. Synthetic regressions cover canonical and alias collisions, input-order invariance and legitimate category/mainspace coexistence. ## Phase 5 — Publisher signatures, custom trust and hostile import protection

- [ ] **TRUST-001** Implement official signing and trust-root verification. **Depends:** PACK-001.  
  - [ ] Canonical signature scope, pinned publisher key, key rotation/revocation procedure and test vectors.
  - [ ] Verify origin + integrity for “Verified publisher”, not just a matching checksum.
  - **Accept:** tampered/forged official pack cannot install or display as verified.
- [ ] **TRUST-002** Show distinct trust statuses across all pack surfaces. **Depends:** TRUST-001.  
  - [ ] `Verified publisher`, `Custom — integrity verified`, `Not yet checked`, `Verification failed` with labels and accessible descriptions.
  - [ ] On all library cards, pack details and install/import confirmations.
  - [ ] Block invalid/unverified installation; allow validated custom unsigned packs after clear confirmation.
  - **Accept:** an imported custom pack never masquerades as an official pack.
- [ ] **TRUST-003** Harden pack inputs. **Depends:** PACK-004.  
  - [ ] Protect against traversal, zip bombs, oversized objects, MIME spoofing, malformed manifests and unsafe HTML/SVG.
  - [ ] Validate origin, content handlers and file-size quotas even for signed content.
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — NFC filesystem path identities):** The provisional `.wpack` manifest now requires Unicode NFC for each object-path component and filesystem-facing pack metadata ID (pack ID, snapshot and origin identifier). This prevents canonically equivalent decomposed/composed names from resolving to the same entry on normalization-sensitive filesystems while retaining NFC multilingual paths. The dependency was already locked transitively and is now explicit in `wiki-pack-format`. Synthetic regressions cover decomposed article/media directories, pack and origin identities, and accepted composed/multilingual paths. No macOS/Windows physical-filesystem acceptance, publisher signature or complete offline pack installation is claimed. **TRUST-003/PACK-001 remain unchecked.**

This closes a cross-platform path-validation gap in the unsigned provisional pack format while retaining harmless names such as `CONtent .svg` and `COM10 .txt`. [Scoped qualification](TRUST_003_WINDOWS_PADDED_DEVICE_STEMS_2026-10-29.md). This is local hostile-path hardening, **not** publisher authentication, offline media completeness or desktop/Android pack-install acceptance. **TRUST-003/PACK-001 remain unchecked.**

## Phase 6 — RAG and evidence-based answer contracts


- [ ] **RAG-001** Current-article lexical/structural retrieval. **Depends:** MOD-002, STORE-004.  
  - **Progress (2026-10-24, partial):** The shared Rust lexical retriever now indexes Unicode Han, Hiragana/Katakana and Hangul runs with adjacent bigrams, enabling unspaced Chinese/Japanese/Korean substring retrieval for revision-scoped evidence and project-aware title/alias navigation. Multi-character CJK queries require adjacent bigrams rather than matching disconnected individual characters; single-character queries remain supported. Evidence word budgets now charge each CJK scalar instead of treating an entire unspaced passage as one word, preventing oversized CJK passages from bypassing the existing budget. Synthetic regressions cover Chinese/Japanese/Korean corpus/title queries, separated-character negatives and article/corpus budget enforcement. Exact code SHA **`cbf69af100fce16d0ae62efa6f65d0b912b079cb`** passed [Rust workspace CI 40079126624](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40079126624), [Platform shells CI 40079126682](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40079126682) and [Android Rust ABI CI 40079126684](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40079126684). [Scoped qualification](RAG_001_CJK_LEXICAL_RETRIEVAL_2026-10-24.md). This is a heuristic in-memory index, not a language-specific tokenizer, production persisted pack search, measured relevance benchmark or real-device acceptance. **RAG-001 and STORE-004 remain unchecked.**
  - [ ] Rank headings/sections/selected blocks and lexical candidates; enforce token budget.
  - [ ] Handle unanswerable questions and revision mismatch conservatively.
  - **Accept:** labeled Q&A retrieves relevant source blocks without a vector DB.
- [ ] **RAG-002** Citation validation pipeline. **Depends:** RAG-001.  
  - **Progress (2026-10-24, partial):** Added `wiki-search::validate_scoped_corpus_answer` for multi-article grounded answers. The caller must explicitly supply an installed article allowlist; all retrieved and claimed evidence must belong to that allowlist and match the index's exact project/page/revision-bound blocks. Empty/unknown scope, out-of-scope evidence or claims, fabricated/unretrieved citations, tampered evidence and uncited substantive answers fail closed; explicit abstention is supported without hidden claims. Synthetic regression exercises authorized two-page answers and each negative case. Corrected source SHA **`bb9bec51b724c96e826f49e509e361644c298be0`** passed [Rust workspace CI 40079562794](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40079562794), [Platform shells CI 40079562818](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40079562818) and [Android Rust ABI CI 40079562743](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40079562743). [Scoped qualification](RAG_002_SCOPED_CORPUS_ANSWER_2026-10-24.md). This validates provenance and explicit scope arguments, not actual user consent, factual entailment, production UI wiring or real-device acceptance. **RAG-002 remains unchecked.**
  - [ ] Assign evidence IDs only to retrieved blocks; verify model-reported references.
  - [ ] Store exact revision/snapshot and jump targets; mark stale citations on updates.
  - [ ] Test hallucinated IDs, unsupported answers and prompt injection inside article content.
  - **Accept:** every visible citation points to actual supplied article evidence.
- [ ] **RAG-003** Build evaluation corpus and retrieval regression tests. **Depends:** RAG-002.

It audits complete indexed shard-frame coverage and title mappings before iterating digest-verified records in stable order, skips redirect records, rejects empty or oversized article corpora, and prevents reports being written within the immutable snapshot tree. Synthetic on-disk fixtures exercise a complete retrieval suite, deterministic report output and corrupted-frame rejection. ## Phase 7 — Desktop Wikipedia-like reader

- [ ] **DUI-001** Create functional Tauri/React article reader/search. **Depends:** BOOT-002, SRC-004.  
  - **Progress (2026-10-28, partial — shared desktop offline mode across tabs):** The desktop Work offline switch now uses a single synchronous app-owned store shared by all mounted and newly created article tabs. Hidden tabs invalidate pending search/title/page/bookmark response epochs and clear transient hits when offline is enabled; request handlers also check the store directly before initiating a new online action or accepting a late response. Existing exact-revision text previews remain readable but are not claimed as installed/media-complete offline articles. Node tests cover multi-subscriber propagation, idempotence, unsubscribe and pending-epoch invalidation. Exact code SHA **`758ce1d96bc24d81c746701376a19fd18ed1933e`** passed [Rust workspace 40684648182](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40684648182), [Platform shells 40684648399](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40684648399), and [Desktop launch smoke 40684648099](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40684648099). [Scoped qualification](DUI_001_SHARED_OFFLINE_MODE_ACROSS_TABS_2026-10-28.md). In-flight native HTTP is not canceled; physical-device behavior, complete offline packs and mandatory media remain unqualified. **DUI-001/UX-004 remain unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-28, partial — article-link label whitespace fidelity):** Extracted same-edition article-link labels now collapse actual Unicode whitespace (including HTML line breaks, repeated spaces and C0/C1 controls mapped to spaces) rather than matching the literal `\\s` sequence. Whitespace-only labels continue to fall back to their validated destination title; Unicode joiners and literal backslash text remain intact. Regression cases exercise tabs, line separators, nonbreaking spaces and literal backslash-s labels. Exact implementation SHA **`a84767952e3c55b462f09b9340f5098a9155de48`** passed [Rust workspace 40681654586](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40681654586), [Platform shells 40681654590](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40681654590), and [Desktop launch smoke 40681654546](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40681654546). This is display-only correctness, not complete installed offline navigation/media or physical-device acceptance. **DUI-001 remains unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-28, partial — sandboxed preview appearance parity):** The desktop iframe's fixed CSS now honors the app's explicit light/dark theme and system dark-mode preference without adding scripts, same-origin privileges, dynamic untrusted CSS, remote assets or new network capabilities. The whitelisted theme flows through App → OnlineReader → isolatedReaderDocument; invalid theme values fail to fixed light defaults. Node regressions verify dark/light/system CSS, injection resistance and preserved CSP. Exact source SHA **`bc98ebae912762b01cd011d02a5671b85a52e395`** passed [Rust workspace 40674405548](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40674405548), [Platform shells 40674405566](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40674405566), and [Desktop launch smoke 40674405448](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40674405448). [Scoped qualification](DUI_001_SANDBOXED_ARTICLE_THEME_PARITY_2026-10-28.md). Theme changes can still reload srcDoc and reset in-frame scroll; no real-browser appearance, media-complete offline rendering or device acceptance is claimed. **DUI-001 and STATE-002 remain unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-28, partial — interruptible desktop offline transition):** The desktop Work offline control is now available during pending online search/title/page/bookmark requests; enabling it invalidates the request epoch, releases the busy UI and clears pending search hits while preserving only already loaded, explicitly incomplete text previews. Late responses and their finally handlers cannot repopulate the UI after cancellation; reconnect also replaces the stale connection-lost notice. Added a manual-offline epoch regression. Exact implementation SHA **`374bac4e868a4a453d376f1719b9c4fe4ea436e7`** passed [Rust workspace 40673392406](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40673392406), [Platform shells 40673392452](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40673392452), and [Desktop launch smoke 40673392338](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40673392338). [Scoped qualification](DUI_001_INTERRUPTIBLE_OFFLINE_TRANSITION_2026-10-28.md). This does not prove browser-level cancellation timing, complete installed offline media or physical-device acceptance; **DUI-001 and SRC-004 remain unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-24, partial):** Desktop extracted article-link **display labels** now strip invisible Unicode format controls, noncharacters and malformed UTF-16 while normalizing C0/C1 and line-separator whitespace; truncation respects Unicode scalar boundaries and preserves legitimate ZWNJ/ZWJ. Synthetic regressions cover spoofed labels, fallback labels, multilingual joiners and emoji truncation. ## Phase 11 — Persistent state and multi-page Settings

- [ ] **STATE-001** Mutable SQLite state schema and migrations. **Depends:** STORE-003.  
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — visible-window cross-process privacy polling and stale-epoch isolation):** Desktop React now reconciles the durable SQLite privacy generation every five seconds while visible, as well as on focus/visibility restoration, without overlapping native requests. This closes indefinite retention of old previews and saved identities in a visible but unfocused window. The TypeScript generation monitor rejects a lower out-of-order epoch without rolling back its observed generation, and the native Tauri preview-cache reconciliation likewise refuses to regress or re-clear a newer cache when a slower SQLite read returns an older epoch. Native and Node regression tests cover stale epoch ordering, unchanged generations and subsequent valid changes. Exact source SHA **`f9390b6b4c92b1f743da121d4f3647da473e8a27`** passed [Rust workspace 40988305702](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40988305702), [Platform shells 40988305744](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40988305744), and [Desktop launch smoke 40988305755](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40988305755). This is bounded polling, **not** instantaneous push notification, Android integration, physical-device validation, or complete privacy lifecycle. [Scoped qualification](STATE_001_VISIBLE_WINDOW_PRIVACY_POLLING_2026-10-29.md). **STATE-001 remains unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — focus-time cross-process desktop privacy reconciliation):** A new Tauri `sync_reader_privacy_generation` IPC returns the canonical durable SQLite generation as a decimal string and invalidates the in-memory HTML cache on observed external changes. The React shell checks on initial mount, window focus and visibility restoration, discards stale article tabs/scroll, bookmark/history views and pending navigation, and re-fetches saved state. A typed generation monitor rejects malformed/unsafe JS numeric identities, and pending saved-list responses are session-fenced. Native cache-generation and Node monitor regressions added. This is focus-time eventual reconciliation, **not** continuous cross-process push notification, physical desktop acceptance, or Android integration. Exact source SHA **`feb215b47a27b94a6f68ca100081925ac7596cd6`** passed [Rust workspace 40982290624](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40982290624), [Platform shells 40982290611](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40982290611), and [Desktop launch smoke 40982290530](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40982290530). [Scoped qualification](STATE_001_DESKTOP_FOCUS_PRIVACY_RECONCILIATION_2026-10-29.md). **STATE-001 remains unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — desktop cross-process personal read fencing):** Desktop bookmark-list, visit-history-list and history-consent reads now capture the durable SQLite privacy generation at IPC entry and validate it before and after reading. A stale queued read from another process cannot return previously captured private identities after a completed reset or consent revocation. Native regressions cover a second SQLite handle clearing data *during* a read, stale pre-reset requests, fresh reads, and history opt-out. This is not automatic cross-process UI/cache invalidation or a proof against a reset occurring after the final generation check. Exact source SHA **`15a16bac4d7836e06e4fb2ebb3d6ae89849f6a44`** passed [Rust workspace 40981774732](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40981774732), [Platform shells 40981774740](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40981774740), and [Desktop launch smoke 40981774863](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40981774863). [Scoped qualification](STATE_001_DESKTOP_CROSS_PROCESS_READ_FENCE_2026-10-29.md). **STATE-001 remains unchecked.**
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — durable cross-process history-consent revocation):** Added `UserState::set_history_enabled_for_generation`, which validates the captured persistent privacy generation under SQLite `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, advances that generation on history opt-out, and updates consent in the same transaction. The desktop Tauri preference command now uses this guarded API and still revokes the local queued-visit epoch. A disk-backed two-handle regression proves old visits cannot reappear after opt-out followed by opt-in in another process; stale consent updates cannot override the newer choice, while fresh visits remain possible. Corrected source SHA **`228c607bf76d81efa35ce9f56d9c0f1edc994462`** passed [Rust workspace 40976573963](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40976573963), [Platform shells 40976573888](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40976573888), and [Android Rust ABI 40976573961](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40976573961). [Desktop smoke 40976489859](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40976489859) passed against the unchanged desktop source from the initial commit; Android codec probe 40976573877 failed on attempt 1 because SDK emulator ZIP download was invalid, and attempt 2 was running at observation. This is shared SQLite/desktop privacy fencing, not Android UI wiring, cross-process cache notification, real-device acceptance or complete user-state lifecycle. **STATE-001 remains unchecked.** [Scoped qualification](STATE_001_DURABLE_HISTORY_CONSENT_2026-10-29.md).
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — desktop cross-process privacy generation integration):** Tauri bookmark toggles/removals, opt-in visit writes, history-only clear, and full personal-data deletion now capture the persisted SQLite privacy generation at IPC entry and use generation-guarded SQL/transactions, alongside the existing process-local queue gate. Added `UserState::clear_personal_reading_data_for_generation` to reject a delayed full reset from another process instead of erasing fresh bookmarks/chats/history; a disk-backed two-handle test and desktop native bookmark regression cover stale-operation rejection and pack-owner preservation. Code SHA **`ec08e162040e61dccbf6cc0e1c18f6c191d45741`**: [Rust workspace 40975751423](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40975751423) passed on attempt 2 (attempt 1 hit an intermittent parallel-initializer SQLite busy test); [Platform shells 40975751555](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40975751555) and [Android Rust ABI 40975751422](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40975751422) passed. [Desktop smoke 40975407697](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40975407697) passed against the original desktop source blob, unchanged by later Rust-only corrections. Android record codec probe 40975751471 was still running at observation. The IPC entry's SQLite open is synchronous, history-consent preference writes remain process-local, and cross-process UI/cache refresh and physical-device qualification are still outstanding. **STATE-001 remains unchecked.** [Scoped qualification](STATE_001_DESKTOP_DURABLE_PRIVACY_GENERATION_2026-10-29.md).
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — durable history-only clear generation fence):** Added `UserState::clear_history_for_generation`, which acquires SQLite `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, refuses an outdated captured generation without deleting anything, advances the persistent privacy generation and deletes only visit-history rows in the same transaction. This prevents an already queued visit from resurrecting cleared history and prevents a delayed pre-clear request from erasing fresh visits after another process has cleared history. The conservative shared generation also invalidates other queued personal writes; callers must recapture tokens. Two disk-backed handles verify stale visit/clear rejection, new visit recovery, bookmark/pack-owner preservation, and an overflow regression proves failed clear retains history. Initial code `6abe303a50b677bdc18b01178e6b5fdfcf327ab1` failed rustfmt ([Rust 40969396504](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40969396504)); corrected exact code **`5080d003e7fdd5707cdd4aedca98d0cc8e0e72bc`** passed [Rust workspace 40969478005](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40969478005), [Platform shells 40969477941](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40969477941), and [Android Rust ABI 40969478054](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40969478054); Android record codec probe 40969477916 was still running at observation and is not history privacy evidence. The older `clear_history` API remains unguarded and production application adapters have not been migrated. **STATE-001 remains unchecked.** [Scoped qualification](STATE_001_CROSS_PROCESS_PERSONAL_GENERATION_2026-10-29.md).
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — cross-process generation-guarded personal removals):** Added `UserState::remove_bookmark_for_generation`, `remove_reading_position_for_generation`, and `delete_chat_thread_for_generation`. Each checks the captured persistent generation in the same SQLite `DELETE` as the exact article/thread identity; an old queued delete cannot erase new bookmarks, reading positions or revision-scoped chats after a different SQLite connection clears personal data. The disk-backed two-handle regression verifies stale removals are no-ops, wrong-revision chat deletion is refused, fresh-generation removals succeed with message cascade, and pack ownership survives. Initial `f581f30fa1f8ef191d0a5afdf4f92f1d220903d0` and intermediate `bf157dcaa8dbee8e6e503be34501f6b22b586d90` failed rustfmt only; corrected exact code **`3eeca56d1a41fc0a1efd86081fd6281d827ed6a3`** passed [Rust workspace 40968459209](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40968459209), [Platform shells 40968459148](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40968459148), and [Android Rust ABI 40968459294](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40968459294); Android record codec probe 40968459167 remained in progress at observation and is not privacy evidence. These are additive library APIs, not yet wired through all application adapters; history-only clear coordination and real-device acceptance remain open. **STATE-001 remains unchecked.** [Scoped qualification](STATE_001_CROSS_PROCESS_PERSONAL_GENERATION_2026-10-29.md).
  - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — generation-guarded revision-scoped chat-message append):** Added `UserState::append_chat_message_for_generation` with atomic SQL checks of thread/project/page/revision membership **and** persistent personal-data generation before inserting an accepted user/assistant turn. The shared two-handle regression now verifies an old generation cannot append to a **valid newly created thread** after another process clears personal data, and that a current-generation append still refuses a wrong revision. The first implementation `f93219519e907841c26b14dd2940dbf8f7ec41f8` failed rustfmt; formatted code `798ac39e60c0d1f993d8495feee0ac35601a3913` passed [Rust workspace 40965813657](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40965813657) and [Android Rust ABI 40965813528](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40965813528), with [Platform shells 40965813551](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40965813551) observed running.   - **Progress (2026-10-29, partial — cross-process durable privacy-generation write fencing):** The shared `UserState` SQLite API now exposes `personal_generation` and generation-guarded bookmark, history, reading-position and chat-thread creation calls. `clear_personal_reading_data` increments an internal monotonic SQLite generation in the *same BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction* as personal-data deletion; every guarded write checks the captured generation atomically in its SQL INSERT/UPSERT, so old queued operations from another database connection cannot recreate cleared rows. ## Phase 13 — Android official/custom pack management and offline media

- [ ] **MOBPACK-001** Official catalog, verified downloads and install manager. **Depends:** PACK-004, TRUST-002, MOB-001.  
  - [ ] Signed publisher catalog discovery, resumable download, real disk estimates, validation before activation.
  - [ ] Network/Wi-Fi restrictions and trust status visible.
  - **Accept:** verified official pack remains readable offline after restart.
- [ ] **MOBPACK-002** Custom `.wpack` import via Android document picker. **Depends:** MOBPACK-001.  
  - [ ] Display Custom — integrity verified, never publisher-verified; reject tampering.
  - [ ] Deduplicate with existing pack and remove safely.
  - **Accept:** desktop-built custom pack imports; shared media remains after another pack removed.
- [ ] **MOBPACK-003** Airplane-mode and A/V-on-demand acceptance. **Depends:** MOBPACK-002, MOB-002.  
  - [ ] No missing images, diagrams, math, tables or reference data and no hidden network requests.
  - [ ] A/V preview/metadata local; disconnected tap shows Internet requirement; connected tap streams per policy.
  - **Accept:** representative math/science packs fully readable offline, on-device search works.

## Phase 14 — Optional local Android LLM, quality gate

- [ ] **LOCAL-001** Select Android inference engine/model through benchmarks. **Depends:** MOB-001, RAG-003.  
  - [ ] Evaluate ~0.8B Q4 model (Qwen3.5-0.8B candidate) against runtime variants and licenses.
  - [ ] Measure memory/OOM, startup, tokens/sec, context 4K–8K, thermal, battery, ABI and cancellation on actual devices.
  - **Accept:** written ADR and documented qualified-device thresholds.
- [ ] **LOCAL-002** Verified downloadable model manager. **Depends:** LOCAL-001, STATE-001.  
  - [ ] Opt-in download outside base APK, manifest + hash/license checks, progress, disk cleanup/removal.
  - [ ] Mark as on-device, separate from LAN/local-network profiles.
  - **Accept:** install/verify/model selection survives restart and needs no network for inference.
- [ ] **LOCAL-003** Grounded offline AI and error recovery. **Depends:** LOCAL-002, AI-005, MOBPACK-003.  
  - [ ] Evaluate article-grounded answers/citations using same RAG corpus and compact prompt.
  - [ ] Handle model missing/OOM/throttling/timeout/cancel; no hidden remote fallback.
  - **Accept:** qualified device answers offline article question with genuine citations in airplane mode.

## Phase 15 — Official pack publication and update distribution

- [ ] **UPD-001** Choose documented publisher catalog hosting/keys and initial release pipeline. **Depends:** TRUST-001, PACK-004.  
  - [ ] Evaluate CDN/hosting/mirror, signed catalog, stable URLs, transfer size, storage and credentials.
  - [ ] Produce a test official pack tied to an authoritative Wikimedia snapshot.
  - **Accept:** desktop/Android clean install can discover and cryptographically verify published pack.
- [ ] **UPD-002** Whole-pack update, atomic activation and rollback. **Depends:** UPD-001, STORE-003.  
  - [ ] Detect newer pack with real size/trust; download/resume/stage/verify; preserve previous version.
  - [ ] User-configurable update check and connectivity policy; no destructive silent replacement.
  - **Accept:** forced checksum/network/disk failure leaves last working version usable.
- [ ] **UPD-003** Custom pack rebuild from updated official source. **Depends:** SRC-002, PUI-004.  
  - [ ] Reload definition, compare resolved IDs/revisions/media, rebuild new snapshot artifact.
  - [ ] Export/import on Android without labeling custom artifact as official.
  - **Accept:** one-click rebuild path retains user selection and validates new source provenance.
- [ ] **UPD-004** Separate binary and content updates. **Depends:** UPD-002, MOD-003.  
  - [ ] App update preserves installed compatible packs/state; invalid major schema rejected clearly.
  - [ ] No unnecessary whole-Wikipedia re-download on app update.
  - **Accept:** simulated app update + older pack gracefully migrates or safely declines.

## Phase 16 — Security, licensing and privacy

- [ ] **SEC-001** Threat model and secure reader/import/bridge review. **Depends:** TRUST-003, DUI-002, MOB-002.  
  - [ ] Hostile HTML/SVG, archive traversal/bombs, compromised signing key, prompt injection, WebView JS bridge, dangerous URLs.
  - [ ] Negative test fixtures and no privileged arbitrary JS/channel access.
  - **Accept:** adversarial tests reject unsafe content and preserve storage integrity.
- [ ] **SEC-002** Attribution and license correctness. **Depends:** PACK-003, DUI-001.  
  - [ ] Article revision/license/contributor links; per-image creator/source/license/derivative attribution.
  - [ ] Pack-level notices and attribution visible even offline; Wikimedia branding/non-affiliation review.
  - **Accept:** offline sample export contains attribution needed for all embedded sample media.
- [ ] **SEC-003** LLM privacy and credentials audit. **Depends:** AI-004, STATE-003.  
  - [ ] Inspect network for hidden cloud fallback/telemetry; log redaction and secure secret storage.
  - [ ] Explicit local/LAN/cloud endpoint clarity and remote-context disclosure.
  - **Accept:** selected local-only provider causes zero hosted-AI traffic.

## Phase 17 — Qualifying UX, reliability and performance

- [ ] **QA-001** Wikipedia visual fidelity + expressive UI and accessibility. **Depends:** PUI-004, MOB-004, UX-002.  
  - [ ] Test representative layouts/themes/desktop widths/Android orientations.
  - [ ] Keyboard/TalkBack, labels, focus, color contrast and reduced motion; Settings subpage behavior.
  - **Accept:** documented screenshots and accessibility verification on supported configurations.
- [ ] **QA-002** Measure performance budgets. **Depends:** MOBPACK-003, LOCAL-003.  
  - [ ] Measure cold start, HTML rendering, scroll, local search, pack build/update, RAM, disk, local inference/thermal.
  - [ ] Set measured performance thresholds and add regression checks.
  - **Accept:** reproducible metrics with supported-hardware details.
- [ ] **QA-003** Integration fixture and offline regression matrix. **Depends:** QA-001, QA-002.  
  - [ ] Long/short/science/math/media/table/redirect/disambiguation/non-English Unicode articles.
  - [ ] Corrupt snapshot, interrupted pack update, unsupported schema and missing network behavior.
  - **Accept:** exact-head test results for desktop and at least one real Android device.

## Phase 18 — Packaging and end-to-end v0.1 release

- [ ] **REL-001** Desktop distributable qualification. **Depends:** QA-003, UPD-004, SEC-002.  
  - [ ] Produce self-contained Tauri desktop package(s) for supported OS, stable persistent data paths.
  - [ ] Fresh install/import/build/export/AI/Settings/back tests.
  - **Accept:** release artifact exists, checksum published and clean-install acceptance passes.
- [ ] **REL-002** Android artifact and real-device qualification. **Depends:** QA-003, SEC-003.  
  - [ ] Produce APK/AAB for declared supported ABIs/Android versions; sign per release policy.
  - [ ] Validate real-device UniFFI, WebView, airplane-mode pack, A/V online and local model when installed.
  - **Accept:** installable APK/AAB and recorded exact-head device evidence.
- [ ] **REL-003** Cross-device complete product acceptance. **Depends:** REL-001, REL-002, UPD-003.  
  - [ ] Desktop imports authoritative Wikimedia snapshot, builds science pack with images and exports `.wpack`.
  - [ ] Android imports custom pack; shows Custom — integrity verified; airplane mode: images, diagrams, math, search, citations and downloaded local model work.
  - [ ] Official signed pack shows Verified publisher; A/V play only with connection; Settings/back preserves page/position; update failure rolls back.
  - [ ] Reconcile every SPEC MUST to implementation/test evidence and mark TODO only when true.
  - **Accept:** full exact-master-SHA release qualification report and no unacknowledged v0.1 blockers.

- [ ] **FUT-002** Incremental per-object or binary delta transport for official pack updates.
- [ ] **FUT-003** Full Wikipedia semantic/hybrid vector search/rerank if evaluation justifies storage/cost.

- [ ] **FUT-005** User accounts/cloud sync, shared annotations, complex research workspaces.
- [ ] **FUT-006** Additional mobile inference runtimes/model sizes, native article renderer where warranted.

Failure and recovery test:

### BOOT-001 — verified Rust workspace bootstrap

- Master SHA: `a466255b478fc4a8d75a35ae3afd93c4f8a39a29`
- CI: https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/37912719275 (exact-head success; `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked`, and head consistency check).
- Artifacts: 9 platform-neutral crates, `wiki-pack` CLI skeleton, pinned `rust-toolchain.toml`, `Cargo.lock`, `Cargo.toml` workspace, README and `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md`.
- Scope boundary: This verifies only BOOT-001. Desktop/Android scaffolds, serde codecs, functional pack building and end-user features remain unchecked.

- CI: https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/37913138904 (Rust formatting, strict Clippy, unit tests and exact-head check all passed).

### 2026-10-10 — MOD-003 record codec host qualification (Android evidence pending)

- **MOD-003 partial — codec choice and compatibility guards:** `wiki-store::record_codec` now uses a fixed `FWREC001` envelope with named-field MessagePack payloads, per-record zstd level 3 compression, bounded compressed/uncompressed lengths, independently addressable length-prefixed shard frames, and explicit codec version `1.1`. Major-version mismatches and future minor versions fail closed; legacy `1.0` is accepted only with `RewriteToCurrent` migration status and is re-encoded as the current version. Direct-offset tests prove a catalog can retrieve one record without inflating or scanning the whole shard. Exact `f89f6a43b1153a044f4deba7bc269d19ee94e912` passed [Rust workspace CI 38019218520](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38019218520), [Platform shells CI 38019218527](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38019218527), and [Android ABI CI 38019218530](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38019218530).
- **Measured Linux fixture comparison at the same SHA:** named MessagePack = 2443 bytes, CBOR = 2444 bytes; zstd-3 MessagePack = 883 bytes, zstd-3 CBOR = 844 bytes. Over 200 debug-profile fixture iterations, MessagePack encode/decode measured 3036/5735 µs versus CBOR 3285/7575 µs. The canonical record codec measured 14430/10295 µs for 200 encode/decode iterations; a 128-record framed shard was 118795 bytes and 1000 direct-offset random reads measured 50973 µs. Linux probe VmHWM was 8004 KiB. These CI microtimings are comparative evidence for this fixture, not device-independent latency guarantees. The Android codec probe cross-build succeeds, but its first emulator attempt timed out before boot; Android memory/random-read measurements remain required before MOD-003 can close.


### 2026-10-10 — SRC-001 verified official-source acquisition complete

- **SRC-001 complete:** the `mediawiki_content_current` acquisition path now discovers only published generations with official Wikimedia SHA256SUMS inventories, records exact authority/project/generation/release/member URLs and hashes, supports resumable HTTP range transport, bounded timeout/rate-limit retry policy, explicit HTTPS mirror transport and verified local-file transport, and publishes only complete verified staging manifests. Corruption, incomplete generations, redirect/authority substitution, changed inventories, missing/truncated members, symlink/hardlink/race cases and mirror tampering fail closed; staging is isolated and does not mutate the active snapshot.
- **Real Wikimedia acceptance:** exact master `6ba82869555a62bfff0169855f0c736b42707f20` discovered completed enwiki generation `2026-10-01` with 19 members from the official `SHA256SUMS` inventory, then verified a real member supports an exact no-redirect `206` range request for `bytes=0-0`. [Wikimedia live-source CI 38021076620](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38021076620), [Rust workspace CI 38021076618](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38021076618), and [Platform shells CI 38021076619](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38021076619) all passed at that exact SHA. The Rust/Python gate reruns the resume, retry, mirror/local transport, checksum, no-clobber and staging negative suites.
- This source-authority contract is distinct from later official **pack publisher signatures**. The governing SPEC requires completed Wikimedia source enumeration, upstream hashes, exact provenance and staged verification here; signed pack/catalog identity remains under PACK/TRUST release engineering.

## Blockers requiring owner action

_None at initial creation._ If a blocker arises, record exact failing operation, evidence, why independent progress cannot continue, the specific permission/input/hardware needed and the next action. Technical evaluations, normal code defects and running CI belong in the task notes, not here.

Initial `1b5257217224081d66f96067a17e15e9f3f109be` failed rustfmt; corrected exact `df3b7585d6da6c5b0d503c2de92c147c2bf6e96a` passed [Rust workspace CI 38095981247](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38095981247) and [Platform shells CI 38095981213](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38095981213). **SRC-002 remains unchecked:** this guard does not establish real-generation import, verified mandatory media joins, sanitized high-fidelity article rendering, or full source completeness.
- **SRC-002 partial — snapshot index membership:** The verified generation's link index now records each indexed page's canonical title and redirect aliases, and canonical chunk normalization rejects unindexed page IDs, renamed titles or changed redirect sets even when the project/generation labels match. This prevents a chunk from silently importing records outside the identity pass. Initial `f11cf7982a96442d62a3edd2ec5e9b4a1c2e7824` failed rustfmt; corrected exact `77f85fe19a1169335e09370c1edd1b909dded525` passed [Rust workspace CI 38096427004](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38096427004) and [Platform shells CI 38096427014](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38096427014). **SRC-002 remains unchecked** for authoritative full-generation/visual/HTML acceptance.

### 2026-10-10 — STORE-002 / PACK-003 exact media-owner reinstall guard (partial)

- **STORE-002 / PACK-003 partial — manifest reinstall conflict fixed:** `MediaRegistry::register_verified_owned_batch` now serializes competing SQLite writers with an IMMEDIATE transaction, rejects changing an existing owner's complete digest/size inventory, and allows identical idempotent reinstalls. `install_manifest_media_batch` rejects obvious conflicts before writing new objects and rechecks exact owner claims plus on-disk object bytes before success. Regression tests cover conflicting reinstall, identical reinstall, independent pack ownership, and simultaneous competing installers. Review and residual risks: `docs/STORE_MEDIA_REINSTALL_REVIEW_2026-10-10.md`.

The source contract and missing integration are documented in `docs/PUBLIC_CURRENT_CONTENT_DUMP_FALLBACK_2026-10-10.md`. **SRC-003 remains unchecked**: live official publication discovery, download/resume, verified staged import, XML parsing, template/Lua/math rendering and offline article/pack acceptance remain outstanding.

### 2026-10-10 — SRC-003 public current-export partition overlap guard (partial)

### 2026-10-10 — SRC-003 complete staged export member verification (partial)

- **SRC-003 partial:** `wiki-source::public_dump_stage::verify_staged_export` verifies every checksum-listed member from a caller-owned private staging directory before import, rejects symlinked roots, symlinked member path components and non-regular files, and bounds individual and aggregate bytes. Regression tests cover two valid shards, wrong project, modified and missing shards, and symlinked paths. Extra unlisted files are ignored, never imported. This verifier requires a separately authenticated upstream checksum inventory and a staging tree not concurrently writable by an adversary.

- **Remaining:** live official publication discovery and authentication, resumable transport, XML parsing, renderer fidelity, activation and end-to-end offline pack acceptance. SRC-003 remains unchecked.

### 2026-10-10/11 — Media notice validation and interrupted pointer recovery (partial)

- **STORE-002 / SEC-002 partial — media MIME metadata:** `MediaNotice` now rejects malformed media MIME tokens, MIME parameters and CR/LF/control-character injection before durable SQLite registration. Qualified code `276264aeea9c59d8fa4c52f3ab592cd1e7f4f571`: [Rust CI 38116511289](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38116511289), [Platform CI 38116511293](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38116511293), and [Android ABI CI 38116511264](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38116511264) passed.
