Regression confirms activation succeeds and preserves those orphaned temporary bytes. Qualified code `8126b36e43d6a513c9511dac7ed98d4091ec9b39`: [Rust CI 38116837523](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38116837523), [Platform CI 38116837527](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38116837527), and [Android ABI CI 38116837529](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38116837529) passed; codec probe pending. Initial commit `a87a8275a55b27312669cc688fcb18931c2209bc` failed rustfmt and was corrected.
- **Still incomplete:** STORE-002 needs crash-safe ownership/byte garbage collection and integration with real authenticated pack installation; STORE-003 needs validated whole-snapshot activation, crash/disk-full acceptance and mutable user-state isolation; SEC-002 needs end-to-end renderer/attribution safety and device acceptance. No top-level item is closed by these partial guards.

### 2026-10-10/11 — SRC-003 bounded public XML extraction (partial)

- **SRC-003 partial — verified compressed-shard XML extraction:** `scripts/extract_public_dump_pages.py` verifies a caller-supplied compressed SHA-256 before streaming bzip2 XML, bounds decompressed bytes/page counts/text size, rejects XML DTD/entity declarations, duplicate page IDs and multiple revisions, and publishes revision-scoped wikitext/page metadata as JSONL only after complete parsing. `scripts/test_extract_public_dump_pages.py` covers valid synthetic revisions/redirects and fail-closed malformed, corrupt, oversized and unsafe inputs.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `9cac7d8f29bf8b9987788ea45374eb43626f3722` passed Rust workspace CI [38117161447](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117161447) and Platform shells CI [38117161349](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117161349).
- **Still open:** Source SHA-256 is caller-supplied and not itself authenticated publisher evidence; XML extraction is not Parsoid/MediaWiki rendering, canonical article normalization, source-to-pack integration, full visual discovery, or device acceptance. Empty-text current revisions are now preserved by the follow-up parser correction at `81ef077ea88b7ed9b8a525c924926f5103e9a85a`, with a zero-byte text regression at `3330faf5831e6244123fba962100a18e523735a2`; unrecognized text attributes fail closed. The combined implementation passed Rust CI [38117567284](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117567284), Platform CI [38117567273](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117567273), and Android ABI CI [38117567285](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117567285) at exact head `1ea990c6dfdf39713577a3d83da14076e43212da`. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**

- **STORE-003 partial — active pointer descriptor identity:** `ActiveSnapshotStore::current` now opens the active pointer once, compares the opened file identity with its pre-open non-symlink metadata (device/inode on Unix), bounds the descriptor read and rejects concurrent file size changes. A Unix regression confirms an existing symlink to a valid pointer is rejected. Initial `3e45a0648f44c2cb31f9c4b2c421cf00bb05d9a2` failed Clippy due to ambiguous `by_ref`; subsequent `defd9498c8a74ce5ad2ff490aed49b9953e480a0` failed rustfmt. Corrected code `1ea990c6dfdf39713577a3d83da14076e43212da` passed [Rust workspace CI 38117567284](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117567284), [Platform shells CI 38117567273](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117567273), and [Android ABI CI 38117567285](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117567285). This strengthens pointer integrity on Unix but does not prove whole-snapshot activation authenticity, arbitrary untrusted concurrent-writer safety, or disk-full/end-to-end rollback acceptance. STORE-003 remains unchecked.


### 2026-10-10/11 — SRC-003 bounded public XML extraction and verified-descriptor qualification (partial)

- **SRC-003 partial — current-content page extraction:** The public-export fallback has a bounded XML/bzip2-to-revision-record JSONL extractor with per-shard project/page-range checks, duplicate page and revision validation, finite decompression/page/text limits, DTD rejection and no-overwrite output publication. An incorrectly escaped shard-suffix regular expression caused valid `.xml.bz2` members to be rejected on exact `26c0cd1cbab2d25156e787bcff2ae82e39abfb7f` (Rust CI 38117824263 failed). Corrected exact `7c4f5befc63de55e8b2c23a1ada335aca5baee2e` passed [Rust CI 38117906021](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117906021) and [Platform CI 38117906022](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38117906022); malformed and wrong-project shard acceptance cases now exercise their actual error paths.
- **SRC-003 partial — descriptor-bound authenticity:** `extract_public_dump_pages.py` now verifies the SHA-256 of a no-follow source descriptor, parses the verified open file rather than reopening a pathname, and rechecks digest and inode/size/timestamps/link count before JSONL publication. A regression replaces the verified filename with different bytes between opening and parsing and verifies the output and temporary artifacts are not published. Exact code `f5b5ff864971f3c988adcd9891a08a7808fa14d8` passed [Rust CI 38118122075](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38118122075) and [Platform CI 38118122132](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38118122132).
- **Not complete:** these outputs are *unrendered source wikitext* and a caller-provided hash does not authenticate an upstream Wikimedia publication. Official completed-export discovery, upstream authority verification, page/template/Lua/math rendering, canonical normalization and end-to-end packing/offline reader acceptance remain outstanding. SRC-003 remains unchecked.

- **SRC-003 partial — UTF-8 declaration guard in current-content XML:** The bounded XML reader now rejects XML declarations specifying legacy single-byte encodings or UTF-16 before attempting to interpret page bodies, avoiding silent transcoding from a noncanonical source encoding. New fixture tests reject ISO-8859-1, Windows-1252 and UTF-16 declarations and accept an explicit UTF-8 declaration. Exact code `174f64136c212774096a72e1c44747e1854d6ed9` passed [Rust CI 38118374656](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38118374656) and [Platform CI 38118374606](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38118374606). This input guard does not satisfy Wikimedia renderer fidelity or offline end-to-end acceptance; SRC-003 remains unchecked.

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-003 nested/duplicate scalar XML rejection (partial)

- **SRC-003 partial:** The bounded public current-content XML extractor now rejects nested XML elements and duplicate direct scalar elements in page title, namespace, page/revision IDs, revision timestamp and revision wikitext, rather than silently truncating `Element.text`. Duplicate redirects are also rejected. A synthetic matrix checks nested/duplicate cases leave no JSONL or temporary artifact; properly XML-escaped wikitext continues to round-trip. The initial regression exposed an overly broad `ValueError` catch that hid nested namespace errors; this was corrected.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Code SHA `979409939644650467c7b3492483614a562ebf94` passed [Rust workspace CI 38127400374](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38127400374) and [Platform shells CI 38127400336](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38127400336). Earlier SHA `8c37a0aa9bc33899d295198f9ef813d3924e8e01` failed the fixture-provenance step because nested namespace errors were misclassified, and was repaired before qualification.
- **Remaining:** This validates local scalar XML extraction only. Official completed-publication discovery, authenticated downloads, MediaWiki/Parsoid rendering, source-to-pack integration, complete visual discovery and representative offline-device acceptance are outstanding. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-003 complete public current-content export JSONL staging (partial)

- **Implemented partial SRC-003:** `scripts/extract_public_dump_export.py` bridges a caller-authenticated current-content SHA256SUMS inventory and privately staged bzip2 members to a **single no-overwrite, atomic-publication, revision-scoped, unrendered JSONL**. It rejects unsafe/duplicate member names, overlapping page-ID partitions, invalid export identity, symlinked member paths, missing/corrupted source shards, invalid XML and incomplete imports. All declared shards are hashed before import and again before publication; the underlying per-shard importer also validates parsed source descriptors. A partial later failure leaves no final JSONL. Deterministic synthetic acceptance checks successful two-shard composition, malformed later shard, corrupt/missing source, wrong project and traversal/symlink cases.
- **Hardening:** aggregate compressed-byte, extracted-page and JSONL output-byte budgets now cap the combined import, not just individual shards. A race regression mutates an earlier verified shard after later parsing and requires fail-closed final publication. See `docs/PUBLIC_DUMP_EXPORT_IMPORT_2026-10-11.md` for the exact offline invocation, constraints and trust boundary.
- **Exact-head evidence:** initial implementation SHA `e2bba7623572f5e2281f80affaa422567ede9526` passed [Rust CI 38132227427](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38132227427) and [Platform CI 38132227451](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38132227451). Hardening SHA `9f6bc709fe046d7692fa84cafa148a9789d04318` passed [Rust CI 38132498464](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38132498464) and [Platform CI 38132498477](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38132498477).
- **Explicitly unqualified:** A supplied SHA256SUMS file is **not proof of publisher authenticity**, a complete export identity or complete media inventory without independent upstream authentication; staged JSONL is raw wikitext, not MediaWiki/Parsoid rendering or a verified article/pack. SRC-003 and downstream packing/offline visual completeness/device acceptance remain **unchecked**.

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-003 structural XML tree guard (partial)

- **SRC-003 partial:** The bounded current-content XML extractor now rejects nested `<page>` elements outside the direct MediaWiki root, invalid/unsupported MediaWiki namespace URIs, and XML nesting deeper than 64 elements; valid `<siteinfo>` siblings remain supported. Synthetic fixture acceptance checks that malformed inputs leave neither published JSONL nor temporary files, and an explicitly namespaced 0.11 export still parses. Initial code `43f2126304ddbe887370c407e25277fbe6affde2` had a mistaken deep-nesting test fixture and was corrected at `81e57c90b66779d99cbddcd51ca56a6c5bf0a226`. Qualified exact head passed [Rust CI 38132943643](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38132943643) and [Platform CI 38132943645](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38132943645).
- **Other qualified import compatibility:** Legacy Wikimedia `pages-meta-currentN.xml-pSTARTpEND.bz2` shard syntax was added independently at `d66473eee7ebfa56198f3131a28447c066c16425` and passed [Rust CI 38133008308](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38133008308) and [Platform CI 38133008300](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38133008300). This is a separate single-shard ingestion compatibility path, not proof that the new `mediawiki_content_current` multi-shard importer supports all legacy inventories.
- **Outstanding:** Complete upstream authentication, representative live source-fidelity proof, template/Lua/math/infobox/reference rendering, article normalizer/pack integration, and offline device visuals remain mandatory. **SRC-003 is not closed.**

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-003 public numbered current-revision shards (partial)

- **Implementation:** `scripts/extract_public_dump_export.py` accepts official public `pages-meta-currentN.xml-pSTARTpEND.bz2` partition names as well as `mediawiki_content_current` shards. The staged exporter rejects mixed publication formats, incorrect dates, history/overflow members and overlapping partitions. Python synthetic tests verify valid two-shard extraction and fail-closed cases. `docs/PUBLIC_DUMP_EXPORT_IMPORT_2026-10-11.md` documents both formats and the legacy SHA-256 inventory trust limitation.
- **Qualified exact master:** `5b5f577a40e38ce5343366649c1dca3b100c3a9f` passed [Rust CI 38134651714](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38134651714) and [Platform CI 38134651697](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38134651697).
- **Still open:** verified official completed-export acquisition, real publication fidelity, Parsoid/MediaWiki rendering, canonical article/pack integration, required visual assets and offline device acceptance. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-001 / SRC-003 official completed-publication discovery (partial)

- **Implemented:** `scripts/discover_public_current_export.py` constructs a fixed official Wikimedia HTTPS `mediawiki_content_current` SHA256SUMS URL for an exact wiki/month, refuses redirects, bounds and parses the completed-publication inventory, and publishes it without overwriting existing data. Synthetic tests cover valid publication marker, unavailable generation (404), wrong final origin, malformed/oversized inventory and invalid wiki/date. Output explicitly reports that shard bytes remain unverified. `docs/PUBLIC_DUMP_EXPORT_IMPORT_2026-10-11.md` describes the workflow and trust limits.

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-003 official shard transport and restart reuse (partial)

- **Implemented:** `scripts/stage_public_current_shards.py` stages checksum-listed current-content shards from the fixed official Wikimedia HTTPS origin, rejects redirects and unexpected responses, enforces per-member/aggregate byte ceilings, hashes each download before no-overwrite publication, and revalidates completed files before reuse after restart. Interrupted incomplete shards are discarded, **not** byte-range resumed. The supplied checksum inventory still requires independent official-source authentication; no full-generation live or renderer/pack/device acceptance is claimed.
- **Regression evidence:** `scripts/test_stage_public_current_shards.py` exercises verified single-shard download and no-network restart reuse. An extended negative-fixture attempt at `2f82e3f56599695b27457fc09d94b92c14d5af10` failed [Rust CI 38136216516](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38136216516) due to malformed test inventory line endings. Additional negative transport tests and full live acceptance remain required.

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-003 restartable official-current shard transport (partial)

It rejects wrong origin/status, malformed range, symlinked partial, corrupt transfer and budget excess; corrupt complete downloads never become installed members. Regression suite `scripts/test_stage_public_current_shards.py` was extended with these negative and resume cases. ### 2026-10-11 — SRC-001/SRC-003 live-inventory revalidation at staging entrypoint (partial)

- **Implementation:** The executable `scripts/stage_public_current_shards.py` CLI now uses `stage_official_verified_members`, which checks syntax and safe-file constraints on a caller-supplied checksum inventory, fetches the fixed current Wikimedia HTTPS `SHA256SUMS` source afresh using redirect denial and bounded response bytes, and requires the local checksum inventory hash to match the live official publication before staging any compressed shard. It fails closed on redirect, missing/forged/mismatched inventory, unsafe source type, unexpected encoding or publication HTTP errors. The lower-level `stage_verified_members` remains available only to explicitly externally authenticated offline/test workflows. TLS and SHA-256 are not publisher signatures, and an attacker-controlled staging tree is outside the documented trust boundary.

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-002 source-generation-scoped deletion evidence (partial)

- **Implementation:** Added `wiki-source::join::DeletionMarker` and `join_pages_with_generation_tombstones`. Unlike legacy `PageKey`-only tombstones, deletion records now bind to a verified source-generation identifier and must agree with all live joined structured/HTML/category records and other tombstones on project and generation. The API rejects stale-generation tombstones, mixed-project deletions, invalid identifiers, duplicate deletions and deletions that conflict with current live components; regression coverage includes tombstone-only batches. This is a new safer primitive; the older unscoped API is retained for compatibility and should not be used as proof of same-generation deletion.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Initial `be2c56dc321c3931e9c586464dea906544032c95` failed rustfmt. Corrected `efc404567cd484147c936fb5c8da15e1ed2f4d59` passed [Rust workspace CI 38150245328](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38150245328) and [Platform shells CI 38150245324](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38150245324). Synthetic unit tests only; no full live Wikimedia completed generation/import acceptance.
- **Still open:** Wire generation-scoped markers into the authoritative full-generation ingestion and normalization pipeline; independently qualify revision-matched rendered HTML, categories, redirects, visual content and source fidelity. **SRC-002 remains unchecked.**

They reject unsafe project/generation identifiers, stale or foreign deletion markers, duplicate deletions, live-versus-deleted conflicts and live records from another project without publishing a partial canonical batch; the canonical variant retains nested reference/table and normalization checks. The older `PageKey`-only importer remains supported for compatibility but cannot qualify same-generation deletion evidence. ### 2026-10-11 — STORE-004 derived offline SQLite FTS5 search (partial)

- **Implementation:** Added `wiki-store::search_index::OfflineSearchIndex` with transactional SQLite FTS5 index rebuild over validated canonical article revisions. It indexes headings and structured textual blocks, bounds query size and terms, quotes FTS5 MATCH tokens, ranks with title-boosted BM25, and returns exact project/page/revision identity. A failed duplicate-identity rebuild rolls back to the previous searchable index. Persisted index can be reopened, cleared and rebuilt without network access. See `docs/OFFLINE_FTS5_SEARCH_2026-10-11.md`.
- **Exact-head code and test qualification:** `71c0eac8f271823f072d2e9b29f11b5ebd22df25` passed [Rust workspace CI 38154971048](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38154971048), [Platform shells CI 38154971045](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38154971045), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38154971148](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38154971148). The earlier implementation `d2c3237fe91460b9ce8e51914268cabbf4592a8d` and test `2d0ce5f2166c6e8c465113ec0992a24c8ae30bd3` failed rustfmt, then were corrected. Android record codec probe at exact code SHA was still running when this note was prepared.
- **Not complete:** No authenticated full-snapshot ingestion, catalog streaming integration, large multilingual benchmark, corruption rebuild workflow or desktop/Android airplane-mode search acceptance. **STORE-004 remains unchecked.**


### 2026-10-11 — STORE-004 canonical redirect aliases in offline search (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-store::search_index::OfflineSearchIndex::rebuild_from_catalog` now traverses verified redirect chains from the immutable catalog and indexes redirect-only/Unicode names as searchable text on the already-indexed canonical article. A bounded two-pass streaming rebuild uses one SQLite transaction; redirect records never become separate results, and all search hits remain exact project/page/revision. Dangling/cyclic/corrupt redirects and per-article text limits roll back to the previously complete index. Synthetic fixture tests cover one-hop and multi-hop chains, Unicode aliases, no duplicate results, late corruption/dangling targets, stale navigation and cycle rollback. This does not require a network fetch; catalog authority/full snapshot verification is a separate precondition.
- **Qualified exact-code head:** `8bc408a722b6380159c3f5c6e74c7ba7bfe58a0a` passed [Rust workspace CI 38161442054](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38161442054), [Platform shells CI 38161442018](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38161442018) and [Android ABI CI 38161442017](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38161442017). - **Outstanding:** Real authoritative complete-snapshot installation, atomically rebuilt/recovered derived indexes across activated packs, multilingual performance/memory measurements and desktop/Android airplane-mode UX/device validation. **STORE-004 remains unchecked.** No publisher trust or offline-ready claim derives from text indexing.


### 2026-10-11 — STORE-004 atomic rebuild of damaged persisted search index (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-store::search_index::OfflineSearchIndex::replace_persisted_from_catalog` stages a new private SQLite FTS5 file from digest/identity-verified catalog frames, closes and syncs the complete candidate, then atomically renames it over a preexisting missing/damaged derived index. Existing index bytes remain untouched if catalog, redirect, SQLite or pre-publish I/O validation fails. It rejects non-directory/symlink parent or output path, bounds create-new temporary path collisions and cleans unsuccessful candidates. Requires exclusive access to a trusted private directory and no open SQLite index readers; source snapshot/manifest authentication remains a separate requirement. Synthetic regressions prove recovery from invalid SQLite bytes without altering source shard bytes, and failure rollback when a dangling redirect prevents rebuilding.
- **Exact-code qualification:** `af9e6220f6cb1d47210a98d0a12f2b1dbf8a44ec` passed [Rust workspace CI 38162290509](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38162290509), [Platform shells CI 38162290578](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38162290578), and [Android ABI CI 38162290462](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38162290462). Earlier `55326a6f857f99e1282824203a509d9519f486c2` failed the Rust formatter gate and was corrected. Android codec emulator probe on this head was still running when this note was prepared, not a production offline-search device test.
- **Outstanding:** End-to-end authenticated snapshot activation/manifest verification, actual installed-pack lifecycle integration, crash/disk-full injection for persistent index swaps, multilingual benchmark and airplane-mode desktop/Android reader qualification. **STORE-004 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-11 — PACK-001 / TRUST-003 manifest object path prefix isolation (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-pack-format::manifest::Manifest::validate` now rejects an object filename that collides with another object's ancestor directory, including ASCII case variants and irrespective of input ordering. For example, `media` and `media/charts/plot.svg` cannot both appear in one valid pack manifest. `identity_transcript` also refuses a colliding manifest because it requires successful validation. This closes a path conflict at the declarative boundary; it does not constitute streamed archive extraction, complete object-byte verification, signed publisher trust or an end-to-end hostile pack install.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `26acf21b0c693ea888813d13db2f399da2ab4243` passed [Rust workspace CI 38170563302](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38170563302), [Platform shells CI 38170563303](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38170563303), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38170563298](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38170563298). Unit fixtures cover both record orderings, a case variant, and valid lookalike prefixes that are not directory ancestors.
- **Outstanding:** Canonical complete container, verified mandatory media and attribution, installer/roundtrip, malicious archive suite, full signing and mobile trust/airplane-mode acceptance. **PACK-001 and TRUST-003 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-11 — STORE-002/STORE-003 external hard-link isolation (partial)

- **Media object safety:** `MediaObjectStore::read_verified` now rejects Unix objects with persistent additional hard links. A second pathname outside the content-addressed store could otherwise mutate the same inode after a successful digest check. A regression creates an external hard link to an otherwise valid diagram, rejects both reads and deduplicated reinstall, and verifies normal reads resume after the external link is removed. The first implementation `4e62add7a2633695ea5ea43e0b3f638f61ecff7b` exposed a legitimate concurrent-writer race ([Rust CI 38188841170](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38188841170)): the no-overwrite publisher temporarily has two links during its atomic promotion. The corrected bounded 500-ms wait distinguishes that transient publication state from a persistent outside link. Exact code `50c3f8446cba13d567282a697889d33b295b4c91` passed [Rust CI 38188963041](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38188963041), [Platform CI 38188962916](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38188962916), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38188962967](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38188962967). The unrelated Android codec emulator run 38188962847 was still running at reconciliation.
- **Active snapshot safety:** `ActiveSnapshotStore::current` now refuses Unix hard-linked pointer files before/after descriptor reads. The regression confirms an outside alias cannot silently become an accepted active-snapshot selector, and a normal pointer is readable again after removing that alias. Exact code `2e35230d83c221ba4b2c887f7d731c46fe3e4fb6` passed [Rust CI 38189427466](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38189427466), [Platform CI 38189427536](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38189427536), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38189427702](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38189427702). Android codec emulator run 38189427492 was still in progress at reconciliation.
- **Boundaries:** These are synthetic Unix filesystem safety checks, not protection against all hostile concurrent filesystem modifications and not a complete verified install/activation transaction. Publisher signatures, all required visual discovery, full object ownership/GC, crash/power-loss tests, official snapshot import and real offline Android/desktop acceptance remain open. **STORE-002, STORE-003 and PACK-003 stay unchecked.**

### 2026-10-11 — SRC-001 source-generation resource-budget guard (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-source::SourceGeneration::validate` now rejects declared generations exceeding 10,000 source files or 1 PiB of aggregate declared bytes, with checked addition to prevent overflow. It enforces the budget before accepting a caller-supplied manifest for staging. Tests reject oversized single-file claims, `u64::MAX` lengths and excessive file counts while preserving a valid at-limit generation. These are provisional per-generation safety limits, not measured full-Wikimedia dataset sizes.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `b04c8bc4a602c3a66d0d0816e0e4ab5c3fd42f2e` passed [Rust workspace CI 38189920553](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38189920553) and [Platform shells CI 38189920533](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38189920533). This is synthetic manifest validation on hosted Linux CI, not an authenticated live Wikimedia download.
- **Outstanding:** Independently authenticated completed source discovery, actual resumable public/Enterprise acquisition and checksums, full-generation rendering/normalization and device offline acceptance. **SRC-001 remains unchecked.**


### 2026-10-11 — PACK-001 provisional streamed transport envelope (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-pack-format::container` writes and independently verifies a deterministic `FWPACK01` envelope: magic, bounded canonical manifest transcript and per-object raw byte streams in sorted path order. Verification reconstructs and validates the manifest, rejects noncanonical encodings, reads objects in 64-KiB chunks, checks declared lengths and SHA-256, and rejects truncated/appended bytes. The producer enforces lengths/digests before reporting success. The 64-MiB manifest bound is provisional. Unit fixtures exercise reordering/case determinism, roundtrip, corruption, truncation, oversize manifest claims and producer mismatch. Failed exports must be discarded by callers.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Code `19dd8c17d4ca8c7e9b655b590fac75f4266a220d` passed [Rust workspace CI 38190698271](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38190698271), [Platform shells CI 38190698280](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38190698280) and [Android Rust ABI CI 38190698305](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38190698305). Initial `3a9b2fe52cd435ebb7ad806eb5f6ddb49653db32` failed only formatting and was corrected.
- **Not complete:** This unsigned envelope is not the final portable schema, authenticated publisher pack, transactional installer or proof of mandatory offline visuals. It lacks Android installer/roundtrip, source/license/media/index manifest fields, hostile archive extraction and large-pack benchmarks. **PACK-001 and TRUST-003 remain unchecked.** See `docs/WPACK_CONTAINER_2026-10-11.md`.

### 2026-10-11 — PACK-001/PACK-004 provisional custom pack exporter (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-pack-format::export::custom_manifest_from_tree` inventories locally staged regular files with bounded SHA-256 and size checks, rejects hard/symlinked objects and unsafe paths, then `write_custom_tree` rechecks all declared objects before streaming a deterministic unsigned `FWPACK01` envelope. `wiki-pack export-custom <object-dir> <pack-id> <project> <snapshot> <definition-id> <output.wpack>` stages a private output, verifies the entire envelope, and publishes with an atomic no-overwrite hard link; existing output bytes are preserved and failed stages are removed. Caller must own the immutable source tree throughout both passes. CLI regressions cover successful create/inspect/verify, preservation of existing output, forbidden destination inside the source tree and symlink rejection.
- **Exact-head qualified code:** `34f5bc8ad97240601eb655f5c0b66358ef791270` passed [Rust workspace 38198835916](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38198835916), [Platform shells 38198835909](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38198835909) and [Android Rust ABI 38198835902](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38198835902). Earlier `f7ee2c7c3d68a622698c9cfb4a41ac7ba4b2e9e5` failed rustfmt; `0eb528979b3260c7ec2e0a8602a87957c13fa8c0` failed Clippy for an unused import; `af1abd9ad4569c384b7c8e6e3db95ad4e14b9c33` failed CLI rustfmt. Those defects were corrected at the qualified code head.
- **Not complete:** No signed publisher trust, source/definition provenance, mandatory visual media/licensing completeness, canonical installed-pack state, Android import or release acceptance. The object tree is explicitly caller-managed; an unsigned custom envelope is not a verified official pack. **PACK-001/PACK-004/TRUST-003 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-11 — PACK-001/TRUST-003 verified but untrusted CLI staging (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-pack stage-unsigned <file.wpack> <private-parent> <name>` exposes the existing two-pass `stage_container` API, requiring an owner-controlled private parent directory, checking all envelope object hashes, and extracting into a new no-overwrite private candidate directory. A second-pass check now rejects appended/trailing bytes even if the input changes after the initial verification. Rejected inputs clean staged candidates; already staged objects are not overwritten. Claimed-official origin is displayed as unauthenticated. This CLI **never installs or activates** a pack and does not certify publisher signatures, media completeness or licensing.

### 2026-10-11 — PACK-003 reverified declared offline visuals and A/V previews (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-pack-builder::media::check_declared_staged_media` connects verified `FWPACK01` staged-object bytes to a caller-provided required-media inventory. It first rehashes every manifest-declared object against the on-disk staging tree, then requires each declared image/diagram/plot/map/math/style/font and A/V preview to have a matching local digest and byte length. Audio/video stream payloads remain online-on-demand, with a mandatory local preview entry; duplicate content-addressed bytes are counted once. Synthetic tests check visual-plus-preview acceptance, absent stream preview rejection, missing declared media and post-inventory tampering.

It cannot label a pack offline-ready. Full renderer dependency discovery, safe SVG/math processing, media fidelity, Android offline use and signed publisher trust remain mandatory. **PACK-003 is unchecked.**

### 2026-10-11 — TRUST-003/PACK-001 ancestor-symlink staging guard (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-pack-format::objects::verify_manifest_directory` now rejects a symlink in any ancestor of the requested staging root, even when the final root itself is a regular directory. Before this guard, a path containing a symlinked grandparent could have redirected verified reads beyond the expected private object tree. The Unix fixture verifies both fail-closed alias access and successful direct access to the same digest-matched bytes. As with other path checks, this assumes exclusive ownership preventing concurrent rename/TOCTOU attacks.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `b4d4d14d641d05b6f35e66be20a57795bd415579` passed [Rust workspace CI 38200706617](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38200706617), [Platform shells CI 38200706564](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38200706564) and [Android Rust ABI CI 38200706569](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38200706569). This is a local filesystem security regression, **not** full malicious-pack install or Android real-device acceptance. **TRUST-003 and PACK-001 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-11 — PACK-004 fail-closed custom source membership (partial)

- **Implementation:** The `wiki-pack-format::export::write_custom_tree` path now traverses the source object directory a second time before publication and requires its file set to exactly match the earlier validated manifest. An added, missing, symlinked or unlisted file is rejected rather than silently omitted. The synthetic regression adds a new SVG after inventory, confirms refusal, then confirms separately that changing existing object content fails digest verification. This assumes the tree is exclusively owned and immutable while checked and exported; a concurrent filesystem adversary is not fully addressed.

The initial membership commit `f36476054748cd81d3654b803dd7e21146439b7d` failed only rustfmt import ordering; corrected using Ralph Bridge single-file CAS.
- **Remaining:** End-to-end authenticated source import, mandatory renderer-discovered visuals/attribution, signed publisher provenance, installer/activation, desktop/Android airplane-mode acceptance and long-running builder jobs. **PACK-001, PACK-003, PACK-004 and TRUST-003 remain unchecked.**

Synthetic test mutations to publication date, member count, digest and shape reject combined output; matching receipt still imports successfully.
- **Remaining:** Authenticated official source release, complete MediaWiki-compatible rendering of complex templates/math/refs, media completeness and portable offline-pack/desktop/Android acceptance are outstanding. **SRC-003 stays unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — SRC-003 Parsoid rendering architecture evaluation (partial)

- **Design:** `docs/PUBLIC_DUMP_RENDERING_FIDELITY_ADR_2026-10-12.md` documents revision-pinned MediaWiki/Parsoid, template/Lua/math/reference dependency and media requirements, provenance, fidelity fixtures and real offline desktop/Android acceptance gates. This is not an implemented renderer.

### 2026-10-12 — SRC-005 XML namespace spoofing regression (partial)

- **Implementation:** The bounded public current-content XML parser now requires every element to use the same supported MediaWiki XML namespace as its root. This closes a source-identity spoofing path where `ElementTree` wildcard child selectors would otherwise treat foreign-namespaced `page`, `id`, `title` or `text` elements as native Wikimedia export fields. Synthetic malicious fixtures assert fail-closed rejection with no partially published JSONL; valid siteinfo and normal namespace 0.10/0.11 exports remain supported.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `ff4a2e9d581bd84df7fc824b04abb72d939ee8d4` passed [Rust workspace CI 38311176543](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38311176543) and [Platform shells CI 38311176691](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38311176691), including the Python source fixture suite.
- **Remaining:** This is a bounded synthetic source-regression increment, not complete official rendering/metadata fidelity or pack/desktop/Android acceptance. **SRC-003 and SRC-005 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — SRC-003 raw export main-slot and parent revision ancestry (partial)

- **Implementation:** `scripts/extract_public_dump_pages.py` preserves optional `parentid` and main-slot `origin` from current-content XML as `parent_revision_id` and `main_origin_revision_id` in the unrendered JSONL record. Both reject malformed or out-of-range positive identifiers; absent elements remain `null` rather than silently inferred. This is source ancestry metadata, not a rendered article or a claim that historical transclusions are available.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `b899a14832d93221ddfaacc95c4d2ffb1a02abc9` passed [Rust workspace CI 38313021436](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38313021436) and [Platform shells CI 38313021374](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38313021374). Regression fixtures preserve Arabic/Chinese/bidirectional math-and-reference wikitext verbatim, assert main-slot and parent revision identity, and reject invalid, zero, noncanonical and overflowing IDs without publishing a partial shard.
- **Remaining:** This remains source-transport and raw XML normalization only. Actual publication authentication, Parsoid/MediaWiki rendering, revision-matched templates/math/references, mandatory media, full offline pack lifecycle and device acceptance remain unqualified. **SRC-003 and SRC-005 stay unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — SRC-005 public XML siteinfo project identity (partial)

- **Implementation:** `scripts/extract_public_dump_pages.py` now validates optional direct-root `siteinfo/dbname` against the export's declared project identity. It rejects mismatched, explicitly empty, duplicate or nested database identifiers, duplicate/late siteinfo blocks and misplaced siteinfo, without publishing partial JSONL. The regression suite covers correct identity, wrong project/case/whitespace, empty fields, duplicate/late siteinfo and nested database fields; older synthetic fixtures without siteinfo remain supported.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `3cd5701e2285500c64c0f96522f89082a81ea16f` passed [Rust workspace CI 38313870929](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38313870929) and [Platform shells CI 38313870920](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38313870920). The prior code head `a5a48922518d391926afe1085272424af6061000` exposed an empty-present-field validation defect and was corrected before qualification.
- **Trust boundary:** Consistency with XML-declared project identity does **not** authenticate upstream Wikimedia source, establish HTML rendering fidelity, verify required media, or qualify offline packs and devices. **SRC-003 and SRC-005 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — RAG-001 bounded provider question payload (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-ai::rag::answer_article` enforces the shared `wiki_search::MAX_LEXICAL_QUERY_BYTES` cap on user questions before building article evidence or creating any LLM request. Previously, oversize questions yielded zero lexical matches but could still be passed to a provider when preselected evidence existed. Unicode-overlength regression with a must-not-call provider verifies `RagError::InvalidQuestion` is returned without provider inspection, streaming or passage transfer. This is a resource/privacy boundary, not proof of answer entailment.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `b8d45ee801b34612e68b33310715fc40320b254e` passed [Rust workspace CI 38315221312](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38315221312), [Platform shells CI 38315221462](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38315221462), and [Android Rust ABI qualification CI 38315221307](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38315221307).
- **Remaining:** Full lexical ranking/evaluation, real provider/UI grounded-answer lifecycle, complete source provenance and stable offline citations are not established. **RAG-001/AI-005 stay unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — SRC-005 duplicate cross-page revision identity rejection (partial)

- **Implementation:** `scripts/extract_public_dump_pages.py` now rejects a repeated current revision ID belonging to different pages in one declared public XML shard, in addition to duplicate page IDs. A synthetic conflicting pair with distinct page identities fails closed, publishes no JSONL and removes temporary output. This is per-shard input consistency, not verification of complete cross-shard revision uniqueness or an authenticated Wikimedia publication.

- **Remaining:** Independent publisher/release authentication, cross-shard identity reconciliation, actual Parsoid-compatible rendering with template/Lua/math/references fidelity, mandatory media, pack import and real desktop/Android offline acceptance. **SRC-003 and SRC-005 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — STORE-001 private snapshot root and shard link integrity (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-store::catalog` rejects a symlinked snapshot root and multiply linked shard files before bounded digest-verified frame reads or catalog insertion; shard-length checks also validate the root. The Unix regression verifies normal reads, symlink-root rejection, hardlink rejection and recovery after removing the extra link. The check assumes caller-owned immutable directory paths; it is not a complete defense against untrusted concurrent ancestor swaps and does not authenticate source publication.

### 2026-10-12 — STORE-004/RAG-001 bounded corpus-wide evidence selection (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-search::CorpusLexicalIndex::search_with_word_budget` applies a single global word budget to stable cross-article lexical rankings, skips oversized passages instead of truncating revision-scoped evidence, and preserves caller result limits. Deterministic synthetic tests exercise three evidence passages across two article IDs, zero/tight/ample budgets, limit bounds, and oversized Unicode-safe query preflight. This is a reusable in-memory retrieval API, not a persisted offline full-text index or a proven mobile/desktop search UI.

### 2026-10-12 — SRC-003 cross-shard revision identity validation (partial)

- **Implementation:** `scripts/extract_public_dump_export.py` now uses a disk-backed SQLite unique revision-ID index when assembling a caller-pinned complete current-content export. The combined export rejects a repeated revision ID across non-overlapping page-ID partitions before publication, preventing two distinct pages in separate shards from claiming one revision. The index lives in a temporary work directory and does not require loading every revision ID into RAM. This is additional consistency validation of supplied authenticated-elsewhere inputs; it does not establish publisher identity.

### 2026-10-12 — SRC-003 duplicate exact namespace/title export identities (partial)

- **Implementation:** `scripts/extract_public_dump_export.py` now rejects two records in the same export that claim an identical `(namespace, title)` identity even when their page IDs and revision IDs differ. The existing temporary disk-backed SQLite consistency index handles this across all declared shards without an unbounded in-memory title set. ### 2026-10-12 — SRC-002 exact-revision uniqueness in component joins (partial)

The generic join remains revision-scoped and does not by itself authenticate the source components.

- **Implementation:** `wiki-ai::local_endpoint::LocalEndpoint` now exposes an exact `SocketAddr` for explicit local/private IP endpoints. `localhost` pins to `127.0.0.1` without DNS; HTTP/HTTPS omitted ports default to 80/443, explicit ports are checked, and bracketed IPv4 authorities are rejected. - **Implementation:** `wiki-ai::sse::SseDecoder` tracks the presence of a `data:` field independently from its string contents, dispatching explicitly empty events and preserving empty lines in multiline data instead of dropping them. Malformed/impossible dates fail closed before staged JSONL publication. Synthetic Rust and Python regressions exercise accepted daily exports and rejected impossible dates.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Rust date validator `fa70a8cf95ab2c61e6af365f0d5bb5c8d5631218` passed [Rust workspace CI 38354189161](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38354189161) and [Platform shells CI 38354189107](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38354189107). Follow-up Python import and regression changes `1108fc7540af302d9992db0401b7756e70d4e8ec` passed [Rust workspace CI 38354462664](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38354462664) (including discovered Python unittest suite) and [Platform shells CI 38354462684](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38354462684) on the exact same SHA.
- **Remaining:** Date syntax and checksum-member consistency do **not** independently authenticate Wikimedia's publication or prove that the inventory is complete; wikitext rendering fidelity, required media, installable packs and offline desktop/Android acceptance are still open. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**


### 2026-10-12 — SRC-003 canonical page-ID shard partition bounds (partial)

- **Implementation:** Rust `wiki-source::public_dump::matches_current_export` rejects noncanonical page-ID partition numbers with leading zeros and any value outside the signed 64-bit range supported by the Python XML extractor. Both modern synthetic/current-content `pSTARTpEND` filenames and official numbered `pages-meta-currentN.xml-pSTARTpEND` filenames now enforce the same bound, while preserving the previously qualified overlap, duplicate-number and project/generation rejection logic. Synthetic Rust regression tests cover zero-prefixed and out-of-range start/end identifiers.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `0c046c693cf7aa40dd383281745ccb7d7bc09e6f` passed [Rust workspace CI 38355102425](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38355102425) and [Platform shells CI 38355102427](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38355102427). Ralph Bridge's original commit response was ambiguous but an independent `master` SHA/diff read confirmed the single-file commit; it was not duplicated.
- **Remaining:** Filename bounds are input consistency, **not** proof of a complete authenticated upstream checksum publication, correct XML-to-HTML/template/Lua/math rendering, required visuals or real offline-pack acceptance. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — SRC-002/SRC-005 typed joined evidence uniqueness (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-source::enterprise_integrity::validate_joined_evidence` rejects repeated Structured Contents reference and table identifiers, including when typed joined records are constructed or transformed without rerunning the upstream parser. The parser already enforced uniqueness for its own input; this adds a fail-closed invariant at the reusable evidence-validation boundary. Synthetic tests verify duplicate reference/table rejection and successful validation after removing each duplicate.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `ba9855506c42b0384e45127ab8b15bec95cc0ed7` passed [Rust workspace CI 38355620792](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38355620792) and [Platform shells CI 38355620735](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38355620735).
- **Remaining:** Actual source publication authentication, complete revision-matched HTML/category/redirect source ingestion, visual fidelity and installed pack/desktop/Android qualification are still outstanding. **SRC-002 and SRC-005 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — AI-002 initial UTF-8 BOM SSE framing (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-ai::sse::SseDecoder` now ignores an optional UTF-8 BOM only at the very beginning of the first SSE line, including when the three BOM bytes are split across network chunks. A subsequent BOM is not silently accepted as another stream prelude. This preserves fail-closed framing while broadening interoperability with conforming SSE sources.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Implementation `9c9d3c9f18e99ac5e13a2f4b03e7b60b3392514e` exposed a rustfmt-only failure ([Rust CI 38355945218](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38355945218)). Formatting fix `ab45b523fa0e50e7c093a0448c56bd4c70d71d61` passed [Rust workspace CI 38356030835](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38356030835), [Platform shells CI 38356030874](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38356030874) and [Android Rust ABI CI 38356030832](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38356030832) on the exact qualified code SHA.
- **Remaining:** This is SSE framing only; real local model HTTP adapters, cancellation-aware socket/TLS transport, model discovery and grounded streaming acceptance have not been qualified. **AI-002 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — AI-002 OpenAI-compatible SSE chat payload decoder (partial)

- **Implementation:** New `wiki-ai::openai_stream::OpenAiStreamDecoder` consumes bounded, arbitrarily fragmented SSE frames and validates OpenAI-compatible chat-completion JSON into the provider-independent `TextDelta` and `Completed` events. It requires explicit `finish_reason: stop` followed by `[DONE]`, rejects truncated frames, unexpected choices/indexes, refusal/tool-call payloads, non-stop finishes, invalid UTF-8 and post-terminal events, bounds aggregate generated text to 8 MiB, and validates optional token usage. This module has **no network transport** and never claims that a local provider is connected.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Implementation `e59528fde54a7f647f3ad3cae0e43b807914bd1b` failed rustfmt only; corrected exact code head `ce484a81e50b0a2e8a210b35283afa8cc57b8094` passed [Rust workspace CI 38358059659](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38358059659), [Platform shells CI 38358059580](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38358059580), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38358059633](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38358059633). Rust tests exercise 7-byte fragmentation, completion and usage, truncation, refusals, tool calls, bad choices and invalid framing.
- **Remaining:** Implement actual pinned-socket HTTP(S) transport, redirects/TLS policy, cancellation, model discovery, Ollama/llama-server interoperability, provider UI and real grounded local-model acceptance. **AI-002 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-12 — AI-002 DNS-free local OpenAI-compatible model discovery (partial)

- **Implementation:** New `wiki-ai::local_http::LocalOpenAiProvider` implements read-only `GET /v1/models` over an explicitly selected `LocalEndpoint` using its validated, pinned socket address, with no DNS lookup, proxy, redirects, cloud fallback or article/credential transfer. It enforces 3-second connect/read/write timeouts, a 1 MiB response cap, 16 KiB header cap, strict status and Content-Length/chunked framing, JSON/model-ID validation and bounded model counts. An HTTPS endpoint returns explicit `Unsupported` rather than downgrading to HTTP. The provider advertises model discovery but **not streaming or cancellation**; `stream` currently returns `Unsupported`.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Implementation `b7e2cc43664dff7e9bd238e10cc39d61fc8b3755` failed rustfmt only. Formatted exact head `c3ec02da3c643ea5acb9cd41c782f7bb34d95ac4` passed [Rust workspace CI 38358434858](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38358434858), [Platform shells CI 38358434678](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38358434678), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38358434672](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38358434672). Rust integration tests open a real ephemeral loopback TCP server, inspect the request and exercise model discovery, chunked framing, redirects, truncated responses and HTTPS refusal.
- **Remaining:** Live cancellation-aware streaming POST transport with bounded incremental HTTP/SSE decoding, HTTPS TLS identity and pinning, LAN/provider UI configuration and real Ollama/llama-server grounded-answer acceptance. **AI-002 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — AI-002 truncated chunked streaming test recovery (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-ai::local_http_stream` loopback HTTP fixture now drains the complete Content-Length-framed incoming POST before responding. Without draining, closing a socket with unread request bytes can produce a TCP reset, disguising a deliberately missing final chunk as `Transport` instead of the expected malformed HTTP `InvalidResponse`. The existing truncation regression continues to require failure *without* emitting a premature `Completed` event; the production decoder/error contract is unchanged.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `7d733ba21ff71ea5732ba82173016a771bb95922` passed [Rust workspace CI 38362006843](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38362006843), [Platform shells CI 38362006806](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38362006806), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38362006904](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38362006904). Prior failing exact head `ec277510a3f0c2a33657dcf6269eae81ddfa0ece` had [Rust CI 38359777321](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38359777321) fail only this regression; the fixture correction restored exact-head green.
- **Remaining:** This qualifies a loopback transport test, not full real Ollama/llama-server interoperability, HTTPS TLS pinning, UI profile selection or live grounded article Q&A. **AI-002 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — AI-002 strict SSE response media type (partial)

Synthetic ephemeral-loopback regressions ensure a spoofed or comma-combined type fails before delivering any model output and a legitimate `charset=utf-8` parameter remains accepted.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Implementation `707ded344cb54c4cc79a7fbe04459959fe8e14d3` failed rustfmt only ([Rust CI 38362542670](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38362542670)). - **Implementation:** `wiki-store::user_state::UserState` now persists a validated nonnegative scroll offset together with exact article project/page and revision identity in the separate mutable SQLite database, and exposes revision-aware reads, bookmark removal and reading-position removal. ### 2026-10-13 — AI-002 bounded discovery read deadline (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-ai::local_http::discover_models` now uses a bounded, incremental HTTP response reader with a three-second overall response-read deadline, 250 ms socket read polling and a 1 MiB aggregate byte limit. This closes an availability hole where an opt-in local/LAN peer could keep discovery open indefinitely by sending a small amount of data just before every per-read timeout. The connect/write timeouts, pinned validated socket, no DNS/proxy/redirect policy and explicit HTTPS refusal remain unchanged. Synthetic reader regressions cover already-expired deadline, oversized response and normal read.

### 2026-10-13 — STORE-004 malformed FTS hit identity guard (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-store::search_index::OfflineSearchIndex::search` now validates the project/page-ID identity with `ArticleKey::validate` and requires a positive revision ID before returning any result from its persisted SQLite FTS5 index. This rejects corrupted or externally altered index rows with page ID zero/negative, revision ID zero/negative, uppercase or unsafe project names rather than exposing them as apparently valid search hits. Synthetic tests inject malformed FTS rows and retain a passing canonical row. This complements, but does not replace, digest-verified `search_verified` catalog resolution.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `eeac13d1ae90e6bfcbe3a86f42291b8370f08f98` passed [Rust workspace CI 38372843457](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38372843457), [Platform shells CI 38372843227](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38372843227), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38372843459](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38372843459). [Android record codec probe 38372843433](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38372843433) was still running when recorded.
- **Remaining:** Full authentic source and pack membership, installed device search, global index rebuild/performance and airplane-mode acceptance remain outstanding. **STORE-004 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — SRC-004 bounded MediaWiki online search metadata adapter (partial)

It rejects invalid project/page identities, malformed response shape, API errors, duplicate page IDs (including beyond the visible result limit), oversized responses/results, control characters and oversized titles. Search snippets are intentionally discarded because they may contain untrusted HTML. The parser validates the complete response even when the caller requests zero or one visible result.

Earlier failures were only formatting and a test fixture that incorrectly double-escaped the newline; both were fixed.

HTTPS transport, redirects, authoritative revision matching, online/offline/cache distinctions, offline missing-state UI and real desktop/Android acceptance are still required. Search hits are not AI citation evidence. **SRC-004 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — SRC-004 exact revision-pinned online article adapter (partial)

The HTML adapter requires a matching page ID, title and exact revision before conservative ammonia sanitization; injected script/handlers and unsafe URLs are removed. Synthetic tests cover Unicode titles, forged or missing revision, invalid page IDs/timestamps, redirect/missing cases, oversize payloads and dangerous HTML. This adapter does not fetch HTTPS, authenticate endpoints, resolve media, or qualify HTML as complete/offline-ready.

Missing/deleted pages, redirects requiring explicit resolution, malformed/multi-page responses, API errors, unsafe HTML and stale/mismatched rendering fail closed. ### 2026-10-13 — SRC-004 title navigation, calendar guards, and fetch sequencing (partial)

- **Title→page→revision sequencing:** `wiki-source::online_flow` now combines official-host title-resolution requests with the bounded `online_title` redirect/normalization adapter and then requires a stable page ID before fetching current metadata. Offline title navigation refuses network work and directs callers to local title-index resolution instead; malformed aliases, upstream API errors, forged page identities and stale HTML fail closed. Initial commit `ea79185f1057e77344d6638aad050cd7433ddc9b` failed rustfmt; corrected `dec506f4ba0dacdb221ff6982249d9c2ee6e6f06` passed [Rust workspace CI 38396879149](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38396879149) and [Platform shells CI 38396879075](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38396879075).
- **Calendar-valid Wikimedia timestamps:** The pinned-revision metadata parser now rejects impossible Gregorian dates, year zero, non-leap February 29, and invalid time components while accepting actual leap dates (including the Gregorian century rule). Added positive/negative synthetic regressions. Initial code `1860ab768d6011f2f992c16c3c8fe3e6ab9e60bd` failed rustfmt; corrected `698c5868c2d992ebad805baa4e027929c84891a1` passed [Rust workspace CI 38397189532](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38397189532) and [Platform shells CI 38397189367](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38397189367).
- **Transport boundary and fetch flow:** New `wiki-source::online_fetch` orchestrates metadata→exact-oldid rendering through an injectable, bounded `MediaWikiTransport` interface. It refuses any HTTP call while offline, validates the metadata before fetching HTML, checks UTF-8 and independent byte ceilings even for noncompliant transports, and preserves separately labeled local fallback on transport or validation failures. Fake-transport regressions prove request order, no offline requests, forged metadata rejection, oversized responses and fallback retention. The initial `a2e392b0d24b913d111d83400aa9f9b7b89c5eb6` commit inadvertently truncated `wiki-source/src/lib.rs` (CI correctly failed at parsing). The full source was restored immediately at `006ad716662ee8d67894471de733d5d69b200fe3` and formatting corrected at **`f61826703c5b724e5c508e9348becdb39eeb42dc`**, which passed [Rust workspace CI 38397948401](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38397948401) and [Platform shells CI 38397948293](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38397948293).
- **Remaining:** This is **not a concrete HTTPS client**, nor proof of live Wikimedia or device acceptance. TLS certificate/origin authentication, timeout/redirect enforcement, real network failure behavior, revision-consistent HTML and media ingestion, persistent cache identity, pack completeness, desktop/Android article navigation, and airplane-mode acceptance remain outstanding. **SRC-004 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — PACK-003 / STORE-002 required-media object size preflight (partial)


- **Implementation:** `wiki-store::media_inventory::verify_declared_media` rejects a required object with a declared size above 64 MiB before reading or rehashing any media. This matches `MediaObjectStore`'s hard per-object limit and the manifest media installer quota, rather than accepting an impossible member size until deeper object reads. Synthetic regression verifies that the oversized declaration fails as `InvalidManifest` while a plausible-but-wrong size fails as `WrongSize` and tampered actual bytes remain rejected.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `5441b55bc19f04a34bc531ec5b9ad39c02f1297d` passed [Rust workspace CI 38459961022](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38459961022), [Platform shells CI 38459961032](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38459961032), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38459961068](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38459961068). [Android record codec probe 38459961092](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38459961092) was still running when recorded and is not evidence of media rendering or installed pack acceptance.
- **Remaining:** A size guard does not discover all required visual dependencies, sanitize SVG/MathML, fetch media with attribution, prove a complete authenticated pack, or qualify desktop/Android airplane-mode rendering. **PACK-003 and STORE-002 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — SRC-004 host HTTPS subprocess stderr safety (partial)

- **Implementation:** The host-only `wiki-source::online_curl::CurlTransport` now drains subprocess stderr concurrently with stdout and retains only a 64 KiB diagnostic tail. This avoids the sequential-pipe deadlock when curl writes more than the stderr pipe capacity before completing stdout, and prevents unbounded diagnostic allocation. Existing response-body caps, canonical official HTTPS URL checks, TLS certificate validation by curl, disabled redirects, and request deadlines remain unchanged.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Source commit `b57427831e0da907fd329f8ae530ff82b7fb466e` passed [Rust workspace CI 38478632542](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38478632542) and [Platform shells CI 38478632453](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38478632453) at that exact code SHA. The existing synthetic curl tests ran; a dedicated >pipe-capacity stderr stress regression and the opt-in live Wikimedia smoke remain unexecuted and must not be claimed as accepted.
- **Remaining:** Host curl is not an Android HTTP implementation; full live Wikimedia interoperability, actual Tauri integration, revision-complete media/attribution, installed offline packs and desktop/Android acceptance remain open. **SRC-004 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — SRC-003 public current-content census comparison (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-source::public_dump_coverage` compares strictly increasing page-ID inventories for an identical Wikimedia project, release generation and namespace using O(n) time and bounded diagnostic samples. It rejects empty, duplicate, unsorted or zero IDs; differing source identities; and missing/unexpected page IDs. This detects a class of upstream omission which passes SHA256 checksum verification. The expected census MUST be independently obtained and qualified with comparable cutoff/namespace semantics; matching caller labels do not confer upstream authenticity.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Implementation repairs through `916ded44aa3497d8061d6178cf0a26108475bd84` passed [Rust workspace run 38484490968 (attempt 2)](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38484490968) and [Platform shells run 38484490990](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38484490990). A first Rust test attempt failed intermittently in an existing unrelated curl subprocess stress test (`SpawnFailed`); a same-SHA failed-job rerun passed. The four new synthetic page-coverage tests passed in both attempts.
- **Upstream fidelity evidence:** See `docs/SRC_003_PUBLIC_EXPORT_COVERAGE_2026-10-13.md` and Wikimedia's published current-content dump specification plus July 2026 missing-pages incident report. A valid checksum alone cannot establish complete source data.
- **Remaining:** No independently authenticated page census has been ingested, no full XML-to-canonical importer or robust Parsoid renderer has been qualified, and the release-end-to-end media and device requirements remain unmet. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**

Synthetic tests cover both official filename families and boundary IDs; gaps within the declared range are intentionally allowed because deleted pages need not have current records.

A filename range match never establishes that every eligible page was published. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — SRC-003 pinned-export decoded shard correspondence (partial)

- **Implementation:** Added `wiki-source::public_dump_shards::audit_export_shards`, which requires an exact one-to-one, ordered correspondence between all digest-pinned checksum members and decoded shard page-ID lists. It applies the inclusive page-ID partition guard to every member, rejects missing, reordered or extra members, rejects out-of-range IDs and bounds aggregate page count. A deterministic two-shard fixture covers valid, missing, reordered and out-of-range cases.

### 2026-10-13 — SRC-003 O(1)-memory pinned shard page-ID streaming audit (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-source::public_dump_stream::StreamShardAudit` validates a sequential decoder's outer page IDs without materializing every shard's page-ID vector. It requires each checksum-inventory member in exact order, rejects missing/extra/reordered or empty shards, duplicate/unsorted/out-of-partition page IDs, incomplete decoder state and more than 100 million observed pages. Three deterministic tests cover successful two-shard ingestion and fail-closed state/range/ordering behavior. This is a decoder-facing validation primitive, **not** a production XML/bzip2 decoder, authenticated source acquisition or independently verified page census.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Initial source `c62e3032f570648eddfeddd044884922fee6982f` failed rustfmt; formatted `2f81fafd2f7794e39bc3da2c67f57659d52ee28f` exposed an incorrectly lexically ordered test fixture. Corrected exact `73862c544927f0680bee1645b597678d472535be` passed Rust workspace CI 38491577069 and Platform shells CI 38491577084, including the new Rust tests.
- **Remaining:** Implement streaming XML/bzip2 decoding into revision-scoped canonical records, independently authenticate publication and page census, qualify Parsoid/template/Lua/math/reference rendering, preserve required media and demonstrate offline desktop/Android packs. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — SRC-003 fail-closed page audit and revision event contracts (partial)

- **Streaming audit fail-closed semantics:** `wiki-source::public_dump_stream::StreamShardAudit` now permanently poisons an audit after any invalid operation, including duplicate/out-of-range page IDs, wrong member order, empty shard or premature API usage. A caller cannot ignore a rejected event and later obtain `finish() == Ok(_)`. Regression tests cover ignored failures and an invalid second member. Source `19ab555e5319fba5c15ce851e4850a62ac49fedd` passed [Rust CI 38493887910](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38493887910) and [Platform CI 38493887863](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38493887863).
- **Bounded semantic page/revision collection:** New `wiki-source::public_dump_page_meta::PageMetadataCollector` reconstructs project-neutral public-dump page identity from an upstream XML parser's explicitly typed events. It requires one outer page ID/title/namespace, exactly one revision ID and UTC timestamp, optional redirect target, correct event sequence and bounded strings; duplicate, incomplete, out-of-order and invalid event sequences permanently poison the collector. It reuses `online_article::valid_utc_timestamp` for real Gregorian calendar/day and clock validation. Source `51a87af3fb70616cca79d75da159c45aef6cc647` passed [Rust CI 38495578613](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38495578613) and [Platform CI 38495578641](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38495578641), following formatting-only corrections.
- **Remaining:** `PageEvent` inputs still require a qualified *actual* well-formed XML parser: this module does NOT tokenize XML, decompress bzip2, read a real authenticated Wikimedia dump, parse revision wikitext, render Parsoid/template/Lua/math, establish independent source completeness, or install mandatory offline media. It is a bounded metadata validation primitive only. **SRC-003 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-13 — SRC-004/DUI-001 synchronous offline gate and deterministic race tests (partial)

- **Implementation:** The desktop invokes both recheck `navigator.onLine` synchronously in addition to reactive connection state, preventing a request in the interval between physical disconnect and delivery of the browser `offline` event. The React component uses this helper to discard superseded online results; it does not claim to cancel an already executing host HTTP request.

### 2026-10-14 — DUI-001 / SEC-001 untrusted article-link extraction isolation (partial)

### 2026-10-14 — SRC-002/SRC-005 signed-range source identity guard (partial)

- **Implementation:** The exact-revision structured/HTML/category join now rejects page and revision IDs greater than SQLite's signed 64-bit maximum, in addition to rejecting zero. Both legacy and generation-scoped deletion tombstones reject out-of-range page IDs. This prevents untrusted u64 identities from being treated as valid provenance and then overflowing when persisted in canonical SQLite catalogs. Synthetic regression tests exercise live components and both deletion APIs.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Implementation commit `a3a4d029f43bbcf828e8c4cbce83a9c18b7fcd70` passed [Rust workspace CI 38537454834](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38537454834) and [Platform shells CI 38537454876](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38537454876), both on that exact SHA. Rust CI runs formatting, strict Clippy and workspace tests; no real source import or device run is claimed.

### 2026-10-14 — PACK-001 / PACK-003 / TRUST-003 same-digest length conflict preflight (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-pack-format::manifest::Manifest::validate` now rejects distinct object paths that claim the same canonical SHA-256 digest but contradictory byte lengths, including mixed-case hexadecimal aliases. This closes an ambiguity where media coverage could select the first declared length for a digest and report misleading installed-byte accounting. Identical digest+length reuse across paths remains allowed; manifest identity transcripts fail closed on invalid declarations. Added regressions in `manifest.rs` and `media_coverage.rs`.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Code SHA `705c61c4e9f61353ef3eeca98405d06f954a948e` passed [Rust workspace CI 38540413619](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38540413619) (rustfmt, strict Clippy, workspace tests), [Platform shells CI 38540413651](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38540413651), and [Android Rust ABI CI 38540413715](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38540413715). A formatting-only failure on the preceding head was corrected before qualification.
- **Remaining:** This is manifest-level consistency, not a cryptographic byte audit, complete renderer-derived media dependency scan, signed-publisher trust verification, portable installed pack, or real desktop/Android offline visual acceptance. **PACK-001, PACK-003 and TRUST-003 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-14 — MOD-001 / STORE-001 canonical signed-range IDs (partial)

- **Implementation:** Canonical `ArticleKey::validate` rejects page IDs above signed 64-bit maximum, and `Article::validate` rejects out-of-range revision IDs. This aligns article/link/redirect identities with SQLite catalog keys and revision storage. Boundary, oversized page/revision and link-target regression tests pass.
- **Exact-head qualification:** `14fcb41b1946edc55e77e1fb0440743f8a79c358` passed Rust workspace CI 38541053484, Platform shells CI 38541053461, and Android Rust ABI CI 38541053416. The Rust gate includes formatting, strict Clippy and workspace tests.

### 2026-10-14 — MOD-001 / SEC-001 bounded canonical section nesting (partial)

- **Implementation:** Canonical `Article::validate` now rejects nonempty section trees deeper than 64 levels before recursion can grow without bound. The guard applies to nested canonical sections, not just the beta source parser; a synthetic 65-level tree is rejected. Empty child lists at the supported depth remain valid.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Code `4f178040807af0eb5857d85449a8f877b305c0b9` passed Rust workspace CI 38541651897, Platform shells CI 38541651754 and Android Rust ABI CI 38541651744. An earlier rustfmt-only failure was corrected before qualification.

### 2026-10-14 — MOD-001 / SEC-001 global canonical block budget (partial)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-model::Article::validate` now enforces a 100,000-block aggregate ceiling shared across lead blocks and every nested section. Validation checks the aggregate with overflow-safe arithmetic before building per-section ordinal sets. A regression exercises the exact permitted boundary, one additional nested block, and a single oversized vector with duplicate ordinals. The existing 64-level section-depth and 10,000-section ceilings remain separate safeguards.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Source commit `2e8c450b67ae4fbfde937c2b7d0f0bc5458a9b36` passed [Rust workspace CI 38543392195](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38543392195) (rustfmt, strict Clippy and workspace tests), [Platform shells CI 38543392211](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38543392211) and [Android Rust ABI CI 38543392223](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38543392223), all on the same exact code SHA.
- **Remaining:** This is a model-validation resource ceiling, not a pre-deserialization memory bound, authenticated source acquisition, verified offline media, complete hostile-import hardening or desktop/Android device acceptance. **MOD-001 remains checked from prior qualification; SEC-001 remains unchecked.**

### 2026-10-14 — MOD-001 / SEC-001 canonical nested collection fan-out bounds (partial; Ralph Bridge v0.6.1)

- **Implementation:** `wiki-model::Article::validate` enforces a 4,096-item ceiling for one nested collection, a shared 100,000-item ceiling across all nested lists/table rows and cells/infobox fields/footnote reference IDs in lead and recursively nested sections, and a 256-column ceiling for tables. Checked arithmetic fails closed with `NestedItemCountExceeded`. An internal validation-budget struct shares the counts across recursive calls.

Tests cover list size, table width, oversized infoboxes and footnote reference lists, and combined article-wide fan-out.
- **Development recovery:** Ralph Bridge v0.6.1 accepted a source write previously rejected under v0.6.0. Ordinary formatting and Clippy failures were fixed; an intermediate truncated full-file replacement was identified and restored before qualification. Only the successful exact code SHA above qualifies this slice.
