### 2026-10-21 — STATE-001 / STATE-002 opt-in desktop visit history (partial)

- **Implementation:** Native Tauri read/set history-consent, bounded 50-row visit listing, visit insertion and clear-history commands now use the separate private SQLite `UserState`. Desktop Settings → History & Saved Content offers a default-off history toggle, latest-visit list with timestamps and stable page identity, open-latest navigation, and explicitly confirmed history-only deletion. Recording occurs after displaying a revision, never in response to search results alone. Saved visits do not contain article HTML. The reader remains functional if history writing fails.
- **Privacy:** The core history write was tightened to one conditional SQLite `INSERT ... SELECT ... WHERE EXISTS` on `history.enabled='true'`; a disabled preference cannot race between a separate consent check and the write. Malformed/sparse/out-of-bounds IPC history records are rejected. A file-backed regression verifies that opt-in visits, disabled consent, and history deletion survive process reopen; native tests also verify that deleting visit history preserves bookmarks. No remote telemetry or automatic cloud fallback was added.
- **Exact-head evidence:** Native adapter `fe251cb7441da188882eb2f18e4f1114ea9a524d` passed [Rust CI 39636238793](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39636238793), [Platform CI 39636238664](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39636238664), and [Desktop smoke 39636238461](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39636238461). Atomic consent code `9b7ae5ee0ef51c16627953856a17a3b4d081c711` passed Rust [39637618031](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39637618031), Platform [39637618049](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39637618049) and Android ABI [39637618032](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39637618032). ### 2026-10-21 — STATE-002 SQLite-backed desktop appearance preference (partial)

- **Implementation:** Desktop theme choices `system`, `light` and `dark` now use validated Tauri commands backed by the private, separate `UserState` SQLite settings table. The startup reader prefers a valid saved native theme when available and preserves existing browser localStorage only as a fallback for the standalone Vite development shell. A generation counter prevents a late native startup read from overwriting a newer user choice; unsupported or corrupted stored theme values are rejected instead of silently trusted.
- **Qualification:** Native adapter `be935162f32292eafb07b04bd6dcdd0b1027f673` includes a Rust invalid-setting regression. Combined frontend/native exact head `f24ebfa2131102f5117dfb4d3fafce307cd2a1b5` passed [Rust workspace CI 39639307032](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39639307032), [Platform shells CI 39639307186](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39639307186), and [Desktop launch smoke CI 39639307161](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39639307161).

### 2026-10-21 — STATE-002 / STATE-003 / DUI-001 desktop reading layout (partial)

- **Implementation:** Tauri native adapter adds `read_reader_layout`, `set_reader_text_size` and `set_reader_width` backed by validated nonsecret `UserState` SQLite preferences. Defaults are `standard`; allowed text sizes are `small/standard/large` and widths `narrow/standard/wide`, with unknown persisted values rejected. React Settings → Appearance & Reading exposes keyboard-accessible, labeled radio options, restores saved choices without overwriting newer user input, and reports persistence failures. The text preview renders predefined 15/17/20px font values and 58/74/96ch maximum line widths; CSS values are taken exclusively from allowlisted enums, never raw persisted strings. Its iframe retains its existing sandbox and deny-by-default CSP, and the UI explains that changing `srcDoc` may reset in-frame scroll. This is a text-preview usability increment, not media-complete offline article support.
- **Regression tests:** Native adapter validates defaults, accepted values and malformed values; `desktop/scripts/readerLayout.test.mjs` verifies tuple decoding, fixed CSS output, fail-closed handling of injected CSS strings, safe HTML preview and unchanged CSP.
- **Qualification:** Native commit `0a6931fd82d6f39c115daa9fe6366129bf27a9cc` and integrated frontend commit `611b1353ff8d13a6537692b7711769c5119658fc`; integration exact-head [Rust workspace CI 39693112954](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39693112954), [Platform shells CI 39693112872](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39693112872), and [Desktop launch smoke CI 39693112819](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39693112819) all passed.
- **Remaining:** Physical desktop interaction and restart testing, in-iframe reading-position preservation, actual Wikipedia rendering/media completeness, complete Settings UI, and Android reading preferences have not been demonstrated. **STATE-002, STATE-003, DUI-001, and QA-001 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-21 — SRC-003 MediaWiki multi-slot diagnostic revision checksum (partial)

- **Implementation:** Extended the public-dump XML diagnostic revision-content exporter to reproduce MediaWiki's primary slot-role-sorted, base-36 SHA-1 hash-of-hashes for multi-slot revisions with text-backed slot payloads. It validates each slot's optional declared text checksum/byte size, rejects duplicate or malformed slot roles, and rejects a revision-level digest inconsistent with the decoded declared slots. Golden-value multi-slot and failure-path synthetic regressions cover valid role ordering, tampered slot bytes, malformed declared lengths and duplicate roles. The decoder does not claim support for arbitrary binary/derived/hidden slots.
- **Exact-head qualification:** Implementation commit `4cf24f40ea4cf922eec9be38a1a784e93cfcb413` passed [Rust CI 39699332615](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39699332615) and [Platform CI 39699332497](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39699332497).
- **Remaining:** These diagnostics still rely on caller-supplied source and expected census identity. ### 2026-10-22 — SRC-004 in-memory verified local-title navigation (partial)

- **Implementation:** Added a bounded project-scoped `wiki-source::local_title_index::LocalTitleIndex` with deterministic title-key normalization, offline exact-title and prefix matching, and revision/ownership provenance for entries supplied only after separate whole-article and mandatory-media verification. A second slice adds `online_flow::begin_offline_title_open`, returning a revision-labeled `OpenArticle::Local` or explicit `MissingOffline` without HTTP, while rejecting mismatched projects and malformed titles. Tauri's native search adapter explicitly declines `LocalResults` until a complete verified local catalog and media loader are wired, avoiding presentation of partial preview text as an installed offline article. Unit tests cover aliases, Unicode titles, duplicate page/title IDs, incomplete versions, bounded queries, no-network flow and invalid controls.

### 2026-10-22 — STORE-001 / SRC-004 verified SQLite catalog prefix title lookup (partial)

- **Implementation:** Added `SnapshotCatalog::search_title_prefix` in `wiki-store` to query the snapshot's persistent SQLite `title_index` by a project-scoped, MediaWiki-normalized prefix with bounded result and alias-scan limits. Title aliases and redirect titles resolve to their canonical article; duplicate aliases are deduplicated by canonical page ID. Every returned article is decoded from a digest- and identity-checked immutable shard frame, rather than trusting the title-index row alone. Invalid project/prefix/limit, directional controls, missing redirects and corrupted frames fail closed. Synthetic regression tests cover title aliases, case/underscore normalization, same-page redirect, foreign project, missing title, malformed input and tampered shard bytes.

### 2026-10-22 — STORE-001 offline redirect by stable page ID (partial)

- **Implementation:** `OfflineReader::open_page` now follows immutable, verified redirect target page IDs rather than re-resolving the redirect's mutable title via the derived title index. Traversal is bounded to 32 hops; a missing initial page reports `None`, a missing redirect target is `DanglingRedirect`, loops report `RedirectCycle` and overlong chains report `RedirectTooDeep`. At each hop `SnapshotCatalog::read_record` checks immutable frame digest and canonical identity. The synthetic regression uses an on-disk SQLite catalog, deletes only a redirect's secondary title-index row, and verifies stable page-ID and exact-revision navigation still work; a tampered target frame must fail closed.
- **Code and CI:** Initial code `861fd2d6eb79a7eb5f8aa5fe6445a6f657b69c18` failed [Rust workspace 39855310205](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39855310205) on formatting of new test assertions. Corrected code `33f3cd32f7809a97661589d0f9ac000e872e309d` passed [Rust workspace 39855495333](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39855495333) and [Android Rust ABI 39855495579](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39855495579). [Platform shells 39855495396](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39855495396) and [Android codec probe 39855495350](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39855495350) were still in progress at documentation time and must be rechecked.
- **Remaining:** This is local page-ID redirect correctness, **not** independent Wikimedia authority, verified mandatory media, complete installed snapshot and publisher identity, production desktop/Android integration or physical offline acceptance. **STORE-001 and SRC-004 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-22 — STORE-001 catalog title-index integrity audit (partial)

- **Implementation:** `SnapshotCatalog::resolve_title` now checks the first digest-verified article/redirect frame's canonical title and aliases against the requested normalized title before accepting the derived SQLite index mapping. Thus an intact immutable frame cannot be substituted by a corrupted title-index pointer during exact lookup or prefix search. The pre-activation `verify_shard_coverage` audit now checks every expected title/alias against the complete indexed set, rejects missing/forged/misdirected aliases and orphaned index rows, and uses a title-index-by-page SQLite index to avoid whole-index materialization. Synthetic regressions cover tampered mappings and successful repair.
- **Exact-head qualification:** The first integrity code `cc88afdaa207755b7a178ee0bffc40f23e2fd5b2` failed Rust formatting [39860236629](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39860236629); repair `9acf0e7274df2af46adc815f1411e438078492d1` passed Rust and Android ABI. ### 2026-10-22 — STORE-001 / SRC-004 exact-revision offline title navigation (partial)

### 2026-10-22 — STORE-001 / SEC-001 SQLite catalog pathname isolation (partial)

- **Implementation:** Both `SnapshotCatalog::open` and `open_read_only` now reject a symlinked parent, a symlinked/nonregular SQLite catalog file, or (on Unix) a multiply hardlinked catalog. This prevents opening an aliased database by accident or following a malicious link during ordinary catalog access. The caller must still control the directory against concurrent renames; this is a path preflight, not a race-free openat/descriptor-based guarantee. Synthetic Unix tests cover symlink files, hardlinks and linked parents, plus recovery after removing the alias.
- **Exact-head CI:** Code SHA `0d041f5e0e6f236767b25e75dbe959271b589a08` passed [Rust workspace 39866446874](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39866446874) and [Android Rust ABI 39866446794](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/39866446794). ### 2026-10-28 — STORE-004 exact redirect-title suffix audit (partial)

- **Implementation:** `OfflineSearchIndex::verify_catalog_membership` now audits the complete UTF-8 FTS body, not only its canonical article prefix. After checking all article rows against digest-verified frames, a second stable catalog traversal resolves every redirect chain to its verified target and checks the **exact ordered, space-prefixed redirect titles** against bounded SQLite BLOB slices of that target's stored FTS body. A final byte-consumption check rejects appended unowned text. The verifier keeps only per-article row IDs/byte offsets (at most 100,000), not the complete redirect corpus; redirects are capped at 500,000.

It proves correct redirect search, detects a forged redirect title despite an intact canonical article prefix, detects an extra unowned suffix and recovers through a verified rebuild. ### 2026-10-29 — PACK-001 / TRUST-003 nonseekable document-stream staging (partial)

- **Implementation:** Added `wiki_pack_format::stage::stage_container_stream` and `stage_container_stream_with_trust` for non-`Seek` readers such as Android document-provider streams. An unsigned `Official` origin never confers Verified Publisher trust.

Output never upgrades an unsigned official-origin claim to authenticated publisher trust. [Scoped qualification](PACK_004_READ_ONLY_STDIN_VERIFICATION_2026-10-29.md).
- **Black-box regression:** Actual CLI invocations accept an exact-quota archive, show inspect-only metadata and explicit NOT VERIFIED trust/media warnings, and reject undersized/zero/invalid budgets, corrupted object bytes and trailing data. These commands do not write pack files or mutate installed state.
- **Exact source and CI:** Initial source `d577d37323c46c0c64788d53c9d3df755963e7d7` failed rustfmt ([Rust workspace 40880959527](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40880959527)); follow-up `7e65591e7dee204cf9fa0df8eda07cef47bc56d6` failed rustfmt on an unintended source-chunk blank line ([Rust workspace 40881049630](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40881049630)). Corrected exact source **`694ee2fd02111b722582991ad574efdbd03ffa76`** passed [Rust workspace 40881156229](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40881156229) (format, Clippy and workspace tests) and [Platform shells 40881156262](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40881156262).
- **Remaining:** These commands are read-only local-integrity checks, **not** authenticated publisher signing, full required offline visuals, Android document-picker integration, pack installation/activation or physical-device acceptance. **PACK-004 and TRUST-003 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-29 — PACK-004 / TRUST-003 preflight transcript allocation against stdin budget (partial)

- **Implementation:** `verify_container_with_budget` now inspects the fixed 12-byte `.wpack` header and rejects any declared manifest transcript exceeding the caller's remaining byte budget **before** the general verifier allocates its transcript buffer. It then replays the header into the original full verifier, preserving canonical transcript/object-digest/trailing-byte checks on nonseekable input. A synthetic 64-MiB claimed transcript with a 32-byte total budget is rejected after exactly 12 bytes of input; no large manifest allocation occurs. [Scoped qualification](PACK_004_READ_ONLY_STDIN_VERIFICATION_2026-10-29.md).
- **Exact source and CI:** Initial `7e79fde528527a501aa00c9a18e2ece04daa1697` failed rustfmt ([Rust workspace 40882454558](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40882454558)); corrected exact code **`a0af3e078c36db8d501c27b92e9a2bcac63a325d`** passed [Rust workspace 40882587232](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40882587232), [Platform shells 40882587233](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40882587233), and [Android Rust ABI 40882587584](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40882587584).
- **Remaining:** This is a memory-budget preflight, **not** publisher authentication, offline media completeness, pack activation, Android SAF UI integration or physical-device acceptance. **PACK-004 and TRUST-003 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-29 — PACK-004 / TRUST-003 nonseekable staging manifest-allocation quota preflight (partial)

- **Implementation:** The unsigned nonseekable `stage_container_stream_with_budget` importer now applies the caller's complete spool quota to manifest transcript allocation **before** the generic verifier can allocate a forged large transcript. It reuses the existing bounded 12-byte-header verifier inside the private spooling tee; digest/trailing-byte validation, exact-quota success and cleanup remain intact. [Scoped qualification](PACK_004_NONSEEKABLE_STAGE_TRANSCRIPT_QUOTA_2026-10-29.md).
- **Regression:** A synthetic 64-MiB claimed manifest with a 32-byte spool cap is rejected after exactly 12 bytes, with no residual spool/stage; a cap smaller than the fixed header consumes zero bytes. Existing exact-quota success, overflow cleanup and corrupt-input tests remain covered.
- **Exact source and CI:** Initial `818249220b9facce9ba3dd4811aff6a9edce961d` failed only rustfmt ([Rust 40885185032](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40885185032)). Corrected **`6311f45e28d295510650280ea8a77d3df315068e`** passed [Rust workspace 40885367706](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40885367706), [Platform shells 40885367617](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40885367617), and [Android Rust ABI 40885367631](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40885367631).
- **Remaining:** No publisher signature, mandatory offline media completeness, installation/activation, Android SAF integration, physical-device or power-loss acceptance. **PACK-004 and TRUST-003 remain unchecked.**

### 2026-10-29 — PACK-001 / TRUST-003 Unicode uppercase and intermediate-directory aliases (partial)

- **Implementation:** The provisional `.wpack` manifest validator now checks both Unicode lowercase and uppercase path equivalence, including case-equivalent intermediate directories with different leaf filenames. This rejects Greek sigma/final-sigma and long-s/ASCII-s collisions missed by lowercase-only validation. Object ordering does not affect rejection. Legitimate distinct multilingual paths remain accepted.
- **Exact source SHA:** `1bc3ea106f7e57b9c58a98c8afec45863cb7fa73`. [Rust workspace CI 40915583974](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40915583974), [Platform shells CI 40915583930](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40915583930), and [Android Rust ABI CI 40915583936](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/40915583936) passed at that exact SHA. [Scoped qualification](TRUST_003_UNICODE_UPPERCASE_DIRECTORY_ALIASES_2026-10-29.md).
- **Remaining:** Conservative Unicode folding is not a complete OS-specific filesystem compatibility proof. No Windows/macOS installed-pack acceptance, publisher signature verification, offline media completeness, or atomic activation is claimed. **PACK-001 and TRUST-003 remain unchecked.**
